<script>
const API_BASE="http://localhost:5000";

/* =========================
   SAFE USER SESSION
   ========================= */
function getLoggedInUser(){
    const keys=["dueMateUser","loggedInUser","currentUser","user","userData"];

    for(const key of keys){
        const value=sessionStorage.getItem(key);
        if(value) return {key,value};
    }

    for(const key of keys){
        const value=localStorage.getItem(key);
        if(value) return {key,value};
    }

    return null;
}

function getCurrentUserEmail(){
    const loggedUser=getLoggedInUser();
    if(!loggedUser) return "";

    const raw=loggedUser.value.trim();

    if(/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(raw)){
        return raw;
    }

    try{
        const user=JSON.parse(raw);

        if(typeof user==="string") return user.trim();

        return String(
            user.email ||
            user.emailId ||
            user.userEmail ||
            user.emailAddress ||
            ""
        ).trim();
    }catch(error){
        console.warn("Unable to parse user session:",error);
        return "";
    }
}

let currentUserEmail=getCurrentUserEmail();
console.log("DueMate calendar user:",currentUserEmail);

/* =========================
   THEME
   ========================= */
function applySavedTheme(){
    const saved=localStorage.getItem("duemate-theme")||"system";

    if(saved==="dark"){
        document.body.classList.add("dark");
    }else if(saved==="light"){
        document.body.classList.remove("dark");
    }else{
        document.body.classList.toggle(
            "dark",
            window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches
        );
    }
}
applySavedTheme();

/* =========================
   DATE STATE
   ========================= */
const today=new Date();
let currentMonth=new Date(today.getFullYear(),today.getMonth(),1);
let selectedDate=formatDate(today);
let calendarEvents=[];

function formatDate(date){
    const y=date.getFullYear();
    const m=String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0");
    const d=String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");
    return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

function normalizeTaskDate(value){
    if(!value) return "";

    if(typeof value==="string" && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)){
        return value;
    }

    const date=new Date(value);
    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "" : formatDate(date);
}

function displayDate(dateString){
    if(!dateString) return "No date";

    const date=new Date(dateString+"T00:00:00");

    if(Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "Invalid date";

    return date.toLocaleDateString(undefined,{
        weekday:"long",
        day:"numeric",
        month:"long",
        year:"numeric"
    });
}

function updateCurrentDate(){
    document.getElementById("currentDate").textContent=
        today.toLocaleDateString(undefined,{
            day:"numeric",
            month:"short",
            year:"numeric"
        });
}

/* =========================
   LOAD BACKEND TASKS
   ========================= */
async function loadCalendarData(){
    const grid=document.getElementById("calendarGrid");

    grid.innerHTML=`<div class="loading">Loading your calendar...</div>`;

    /*
      IMPORTANT:
      We do NOT redirect to index.html if the
      session is missing. This fixes the issue
      where Calendar immediately returned to Login.
    */
    if(!currentUserEmail){
        console.warn("No user email found in session.");
        calendarEvents=[];
        renderCalendar();
        renderSelectedEvents();
        renderUpcomingEvents();
        return;
    }

    try{
        const response=await fetch(
            `${API_BASE}/api/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(currentUserEmail)}`
        );

        if(!response.ok){
            throw new Error(`Server returned ${response.status}`);
        }

        const data=await response.json();

        let tasks=[];

        if(Array.isArray(data)){
            tasks=data;
        }else if(data && Array.isArray(data.tasks)){
            tasks=data.tasks;
        }

        calendarEvents=tasks
            .filter(Boolean)
            .map((task,index)=>{
                const taskDate=normalizeTaskDate(
                    task.task_date ||
                    task.taskDate ||
                    task.date ||
                    task.dueDate
                );

                if(!taskDate) return null;

                const priority=String(task.priority||"").toLowerCase();
                const status=String(task.status||"").toLowerCase();

                let type="task";

                if(priority==="high" || priority==="urgent"){
                    type="deadline";
                }

                if(status==="completed" || status==="done"){
                    type="academic";
                }

                return {
                    id:String(
                        task.id ||
                        task._id ||
                        `task-${taskDate}-${index}`
                    ),
                    title:task.name || task.title || "Task",
                    date:taskDate,
                    time:task.task_time || task.taskTime || task.time || task.dueTime || "",
                    type,
                    subject:task.subject || "",
                    description:task.description || task.details || "",
                    status:task.status || "",
                    priority:task.priority || "",
                    source:"backend"
                };
            })
            .filter(Boolean);

        renderCalendar();
        renderSelectedEvents();
        renderUpcomingEvents();

    }catch(error){
        console.error("Calendar loading error:",error);

        grid.innerHTML=`
            <div class="error-message">
                Unable to load your calendar data.
                <br><br>
                Please make sure the DueMate backend server
                is running on port 5000.
            </div>
        `;

        document.getElementById("selectedEvents").innerHTML=
            `<div class="empty-message">Calendar data could not be loaded.</div>`;

        document.getElementById("upcomingEvents").innerHTML=
            `<div class="empty-message">Calendar data could not be loaded.</div>`;
    }
}

function getEventIcon(type){
    if(type==="task") return "ðŸ“";
    if(type==="deadline") return "â°";
    if(type==="class") return "ðŸ“š";
    return "ðŸŽ“";
}

/* =========================
   CALENDAR RENDERING
   ========================= */
function renderCalendar(){
    const grid=document.getElementById("calendarGrid");
    grid.innerHTML="";

    const year=currentMonth.getFullYear();
    const month=currentMonth.getMonth();

    document.getElementById("monthTitle").textContent=
        currentMonth.toLocaleDateString(undefined,{
            month:"long",
            year:"numeric"
        });

    const firstDay=new Date(year,month,1).getDay();
    const daysInMonth=new Date(year,month+1,0).getDate();
    const daysPrevious=new Date(year,month,0).getDate();

    for(let i=firstDay-1;i>=0;i--){
        createCalendarDay(
            new Date(year,month-1,daysPrevious-i),
            true
        );
    }

    for(let day=1;day<=daysInMonth;day++){
        createCalendarDay(
            new Date(year,month,day),
            false
        );
    }

    const total=firstDay+daysInMonth;
    const remaining=total%7===0?0:7-(total%7);

    for(let day=1;day<=remaining;day++){
        createCalendarDay(
            new Date(year,month+1,day),
            true
        );
    }
}

function createCalendarDay(date,otherMonth){
    const grid=document.getElementById("calendarGrid");
    const day=document.createElement("div");
    const dateString=formatDate(date);

    day.className="calendar-day";

    if(otherMonth) day.classList.add("other-month");
    if(dateString===formatDate(today)) day.classList.add("today");
    if(dateString===selectedDate) day.classList.add("selected");

    day.innerHTML=`
        <div class="day-number">${date.getDate()}</div>
        <div class="events"></div>
    `;

    day.addEventListener("click",()=>{
        selectedDate=dateString;
        renderCalendar();
        renderSelectedEvents();
    });

    const events=calendarEvents.filter(e=>e.date===dateString);
    const container=day.querySelector(".events");

    events.slice(0,4).forEach(event=>{
        const element=document.createElement("div");

        element.className=`event ${event.type}`;
        element.textContent=
            `${getEventIcon(event.type)} ${event.title}`;
        element.title=event.title;

        element.addEventListener("click",clickEvent=>{
            clickEvent.stopPropagation();
            openEventDetails(event.id);
        });

        container.appendChild(element);
    });

    if(events.length>4){
        const more=document.createElement("div");
        more.className="event academic";
        more.textContent=`+${events.length-4} more`;
        container.appendChild(more);
    }

    grid.appendChild(day);
}

function changeMonth(amount){
    currentMonth=new Date(
        currentMonth.getFullYear(),
        currentMonth.getMonth()+amount,
        1
    );
    renderCalendar();
}

function goToday(){
    currentMonth=new Date(today.getFullYear(),today.getMonth(),1);
    selectedDate=formatDate(today);
    renderCalendar();
    renderSelectedEvents();
}

/* =========================
   SELECTED EVENTS
   ========================= */
function renderSelectedEvents(){
    const text=document.getElementById("selectedDateText");
    const container=document.getElementById("selectedEvents");

    text.textContent=displayDate(selectedDate);
    container.innerHTML="";

    const events=calendarEvents.filter(
        event=>event.date===selectedDate
    );

    if(events.length===0){
        container.innerHTML=
            `<div class="empty-message">No events for this date.</div>`;
        return;
    }

    events.forEach(event=>{
        const item=document.createElement("div");
        item.className=`list-event ${event.type}`;

        item.innerHTML=`
            <div class="list-info">
                <div class="list-title">
                    ${getEventIcon(event.type)}
                    ${escapeHTML(event.title)}
                </div>
                <div class="list-meta">
                    ${event.time ? escapeHTML(event.time) : "No time"}
                    ${event.subject ? " â€¢ "+escapeHTML(event.subject) : ""}
                </div>
            </div>
            <span>â€º</span>
        `;

        item.addEventListener("click",()=>{
            openEventDetails(event.id);
        });

        container.appendChild(item);
    });
}

/* =========================
   UPCOMING EVENTS
   ========================= */
function renderUpcomingEvents(){
    const container=document.getElementById("upcomingEvents");
    container.innerHTML="";

    const todayString=formatDate(today);

    const upcoming=calendarEvents
        .filter(event=>event.date>=todayString)
        .sort((a,b)=>{
            return (
                `${a.date} ${a.time||""}`
            ).localeCompare(
                `${b.date} ${b.time||""}`
            );
        })
        .slice(0,5);

    if(upcoming.length===0){
        container.innerHTML=
            `<div class="empty-message">No upcoming events.</div>`;
        return;
    }

    upcoming.forEach(event=>{
        const item=document.createElement("div");
        item.className=`list-event ${event.type}`;

        item.innerHTML=`
            <div class="list-info">
                <div class="list-title">
                    ${getEventIcon(event.type)}
                    ${escapeHTML(event.title)}
                </div>
                <div class="list-meta">
                    ${displayDate(event.date)}
                    ${event.time ? " â€¢ "+escapeHTML(event.time) : ""}
                </div>
            </div>
            <span>â€º</span>
        `;

        item.addEventListener("click",()=>{
            openEventDetails(event.id);
        });

        container.appendChild(item);
    });
}

/* =========================
   ADD EVENT
   ========================= */
function openEventModal(){
    const form=document.getElementById("eventForm");

    form.reset();

    document.getElementById("eventDate").value=
        selectedDate || formatDate(today);

    document.getElementById("eventModal").classList.add("show");

    setTimeout(()=>{
        document.getElementById("eventTitle").focus();
    },100);
}

function closeEventModal(){
    document.getElementById("eventModal").classList.remove("show");
}

document.getElementById("eventForm").addEventListener("submit",event=>{
    event.preventDefault();

    const title=document.getElementById("eventTitle").value.trim();
    const date=document.getElementById("eventDate").value;
    const time=document.getElementById("eventTime").value;
    const type=document.getElementById("eventType").value;
    const subject=document.getElementById("eventSubject").value.trim();
    const description=document.getElementById("eventDescription").value.trim();

    if(!title || !date) return;

    calendarEvents.push({
        id:`calendar-${Date.now()}`,
        title,
        date,
        time,
        type,
        subject,
        description,
        priority:"",
        source:"calendar"
    });

    selectedDate=date;

    const selected=new Date(date+"T00:00:00");

    currentMonth=new Date(
        selected.getFullYear(),
        selected.getMonth(),
        1
    );

    renderCalendar();
    renderSelectedEvents();
    renderUpcomingEvents();
    closeEventModal();
});

/* =========================
   EVENT DETAILS
   ========================= */
function openEventDetails(id){
    const event=calendarEvents.find(item=>item.id===id);

    if(!event) return;

    const container=document.getElementById("eventDetails");

    container.innerHTML=`
        <div class="details-title">
            ${getEventIcon(event.type)}
            ${escapeHTML(event.title)}
        </div>

        <div class="details-date">
            ${displayDate(event.date)}
        </div>

        <div class="detail-row">
            <span class="detail-label">Type</span>
            <span>${escapeHTML(capitalize(event.type))}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="detail-row">
            <span class="detail-label">Time</span>
            <span>${event.time ? escapeHTML(event.time) : "Not specified"}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="detail-row">
            <span class="detail-label">Subject</span>
            <span>${event.subject ? escapeHTML(event.subject) : "Not specified"}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="detail-row">
            <span class="detail-label">Priority</span>
            <span>${event.priority ? escapeHTML(event.priority) : "Not specified"}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="detail-row">
            <span class="detail-label">Description</span>
            <span>${event.description ? escapeHTML(event.description) : "No description"}</span>
        </div>

        <button class="delete-event" type="button"
            onclick="deleteEvent('${escapeAttribute(event.id)}')">
            ðŸ—‘ï¸ Delete Event
        </button>
    `;

    document.getElementById("detailsModal").classList.add("show");
}

function closeDetailsModal(){
    document.getElementById("detailsModal").classList.remove("show");
}

function deleteEvent(id){
    const event=calendarEvents.find(item=>item.id===id);

    if(!event) return;

    if(event.source==="backend"){
        alert(
            "This event comes from your Tasks page. Please delete or edit it from the Tasks page."
        );
        return;
    }

    if(!confirm(`Delete "${event.title}"?`)) return;

    calendarEvents=calendarEvents.filter(item=>item.id!==id);

    closeDetailsModal();
    renderCalendar();
    renderSelectedEvents();
    renderUpcomingEvents();
}

/* =========================
   SECURITY HELPERS
   ========================= */
function escapeHTML(value){
    const div=document.createElement("div");
    div.textContent=String(value??"");
    return div.innerHTML;
}

function escapeAttribute(value){
    return String(value??"")
        .replace(/\\/g,"\\\\")
        .replace(/'/g,"\\'");
}

function capitalize(value){
    if(!value) return "";
    return value.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+value.slice(1);
}

/* =========================
   MODAL CONTROLS
   ========================= */
document.getElementById("eventModal").addEventListener("click",event=>{
    if(event.target===event.currentTarget){
        closeEventModal();
    }
});

document.getElementById("detailsModal").addEventListener("click",event=>{
    if(event.target===event.currentTarget){
        closeDetailsModal();
    }
});

document.addEventListener("keydown",event=>{
    if(event.key==="Escape"){
        closeEventModal();
        closeDetailsModal();
    }
});

/* =========================
   NAVIGATION
   ========================= */
function goDashboard(){
    window.location.href="dashboard.html";
}

function goNotifications(){
    window.location.href="notifications.html";
}

/* =========================
   SYSTEM THEME LISTENER
   ========================= */
const colorScheme=window.matchMedia(
    "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
);

if(colorScheme.addEventListener){
    colorScheme.addEventListener("change",()=>{
        const saved=localStorage.getItem("duemate-theme")||"system";

        if(saved==="system"){
            applySavedTheme();
        }
    });
}

/* =========================
   INITIALIZE
   ========================= */
updateCurrentDate();
renderCalendar();
renderSelectedEvents();
renderUpcomingEvents();
loadCalendarData();
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>

        /* =====================================================
           THEME
           ===================================================== */

        function applySavedTheme() {

            const savedTheme =
                localStorage.getItem("duemate-theme") ||
                "light";

            document.body.classList.toggle(
                "dark",
                savedTheme === "dark"
            );
        }

        applySavedTheme();


        /* =====================================================
           USER
           ===================================================== */

        function getCurrentUserEmail() {

            return (
                sessionStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-user-email"
                ) ||
                sessionStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-user"
                ) ||
                ""
            ).trim().toLowerCase();
        }


        function getStoredUserName() {

            return (
                sessionStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-user-name"
                ) || ""
            );
        }


        /* =====================================================
           DATES
           ===================================================== */

        function formatDate(date) {

            return date.toLocaleDateString(
                undefined,
                {
                    weekday: "long",
                    day: "numeric",
                    month: "long",
                    year: "numeric"
                }
            );
        }


        function formatShortDate(date) {

            return date.toLocaleDateString(
                undefined,
                {
                    day: "numeric",
                    month: "short",
                    year: "numeric"
                }
            );
        }


        function getDateKey(date) {

            const year =
                date.getFullYear();

            const month =
                String(
                    date.getMonth() + 1
                ).padStart(2,"0");

            const day =
                String(
                    date.getDate()
                ).padStart(2,"0");

            return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
        }


        function updateDates() {

            const today =
                new Date();

            const tomorrow =
                new Date(today);

            tomorrow.setDate(
                today.getDate() + 1
            );


            document.getElementById(
                "todayLabel"
            ).textContent =
                formatDate(today);


            document.getElementById(
                "tomorrowLabel"
            ).textContent =
                formatDate(tomorrow);
        }


        updateDates();


        /* =====================================================
           GREETING
           ===================================================== */

        function updateGreeting() {

            const hour =
                new Date().getHours();

            let greeting;

            if (hour < 12) {

                greeting =
                    "Good morning! â˜€ï¸";

            } else if (hour < 17) {

                greeting =
                    "Good afternoon! ðŸŒ¤ï¸";

            } else {

                greeting =
                    "Good evening! ðŸŒ™";
            }


            const userName =
                getStoredUserName();


            const title =
                document.getElementById(
                    "welcomeTitle"
                );


            if (userName) {

                title.textContent =
                    `${greeting} ${userName} ðŸ‘‹`;

            } else {

                title.textContent =
                    greeting;
            }
        }


        updateGreeting();


        /* =====================================================
           DRAWER
           ===================================================== */

        function openDrawer() {

            document
                .getElementById("drawer")
                .classList.add("show");

            document
                .getElementById("drawerOverlay")
                .classList.add("show");

            document.body.style.overflow =
                "hidden";
        }


        function closeDrawer() {

            document
                .getElementById("drawer")
                .classList.remove("show");

            document
                .getElementById("drawerOverlay")
                .classList.remove("show");

            document.body.style.overflow =
                "";
        }


        /* =====================================================
           NAVIGATION
           ===================================================== */

        function openCalendar() {

            window.location.href =
                "calendar.html";
        }


        function openNotifications() {

            window.location.href =
                "notifications.html";
        }


        function goTo(page) {

            if (!page) {
                return;
            }

            window.location.href =
                page;
        }


        /* =====================================================
           LOGOUT
           ===================================================== */

        function logout() {

            const confirmed =
                confirm(
                    "Are you sure you want to logout?"
                );

            if (!confirmed) {
                return;
            }


            sessionStorage.removeItem(
                "duemate-user"
            );

            sessionStorage.removeItem(
                "duemate-user-name"
            );

            sessionStorage.removeItem(
                "duemate-user-email"
            );


            window.location.href =
                "index.html";
        }


        /* =====================================================
           SUBJECT STORAGE
           ===================================================== */

        function getSubjectStorageKey() {

            const email =
                getCurrentUserEmail();

            if (!email) {

                return "duemate-subjects-guest";
            }

            return (
                "duemate-subjects-" +
                email
            );
        }


        function getSubjects() {

            try {

                const saved =
                    localStorage.getItem(
                        getSubjectStorageKey()
                    );

                if (!saved) {
                    return [];
                }

                const subjects =
                    JSON.parse(saved);

                return Array.isArray(subjects)
                    ? subjects
                    : [];

            } catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "Unable to load subjects:",
                    error
                );

                return [];
            }
        }


        function saveSubjects(subjects) {

            localStorage.setItem(
                getSubjectStorageKey(),
                JSON.stringify(subjects)
            );
        }


        /* =====================================================
           SUBJECT MANAGEMENT
           ===================================================== */

        let selectedSubjectColor =
            "#756cff";

        let editingSubjectId =
            null;


        function renderSubjects() {

            const grid =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectsGrid"
                );


            const subjects =
                getSubjects();


            grid.innerHTML =
                "";


            if (subjects.length === 0) {

                grid.innerHTML = `

                    <div class="subjects-empty">

                        <div class="subjects-empty-icon">
                            ðŸ“š
                        </div>

                        <h4>
                            No subjects added yet
                        </h4>

                        <p>
                            Add your subjects here.
                            They can be reused across DueMate.
                        </p>

                    </div>

                `;

                return;
            }


            subjects.forEach(
                function(subject) {

                    const item =
                        document.createElement(
                            "div"
                        );

                    item.className =
                        "subject-item";


                    const color =
                        subject.color ||
                        "#756cff";


                    item.innerHTML = `

                        <div class="subject-actions">

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                class="subject-action"
                                title="Edit subject">

                                âœï¸

                            </button>

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                class="subject-action delete"
                                title="Delete subject">

                                Ã—

                            </button>

                        </div>


                        <div
                            class="subject-color"
                            style="
                                background:${escapeHTML(color)};
                                color:${escapeHTML(color)};
                            ">

                        </div>


                        <div class="subject-name">
                            ${escapeHTML(subject.name)}
                        </div>

                    `;


                    const buttons =
                        item.querySelectorAll(
                            ".subject-action"
                        );


                    buttons[0].addEventListener(
                        "click",
                        function() {

                            editSubject(
                                subject.id
                            );

                        }
                    );


                    buttons[1].addEventListener(
                        "click",
                        function() {

                            deleteSubject(
                                subject.id
                            );

                        }
                    );


                    grid.appendChild(item);

                }
            );
        }


        function openSubjectModal(
            subjectId = null
        ) {

            const modal =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectModal"
                );

            const form =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectForm"
                );

            const title =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectModalTitle"
                );

            const saveButton =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectSaveButton"
                );


            editingSubjectId =
                subjectId;


            form.reset();


            selectedSubjectColor =
                "#756cff";


            document
                .querySelectorAll(
                    ".color-option"
                )
                .forEach(
                    function(button,index) {

                        button.classList.toggle(
                            "selected",
                            index === 0
                        );
                    }
                );


            if (subjectId) {

                const subject =
                    getSubjects().find(
                        function(item) {

                            return (
                                item.id ===
                                subjectId
                            );

                        }
                    );


                if (subject) {

                    document.getElementById(
                        "subjectName"
                    ).value =
                        subject.name;


                    selectedSubjectColor =
                        subject.color ||
                        "#756cff";


                    document
                        .querySelectorAll(
                            ".color-option"
                        )
                        .forEach(
                            function(button) {

                                button.classList.toggle(
                                    "selected",
                                    button.dataset.color ===
                                    selectedSubjectColor
                                );

                            }
                        );


                    title.textContent =
                        "Edit Subject";

                    saveButton.textContent =
                        "Save Changes";
                }

            } else {

                title.textContent =
                    "Add Subject";

                saveButton.textContent =
                    "Add Subject";
            }


            modal.classList.add("show");


            setTimeout(
                function() {

                    document
                        .getElementById(
                            "subjectName"
                        )
                        .focus();

                },
                100
            );
        }


        function editSubject(id) {

            if (!id) {
                return;
            }

            openSubjectModal(id);
        }


        function closeSubjectModal() {

            document
                .getElementById(
                    "subjectModal"
                )
                .classList.remove("show");

            editingSubjectId =
                null;
        }


        function handleModalBackground(event) {

            if (
                event.target &&
                event.target.id ===
                    "subjectModal"
            ) {

                closeSubjectModal();
            }
        }


        function selectSubjectColor(button) {

            if (!button) {
                return;
            }


            selectedSubjectColor =
                button.dataset.color ||
                "#756cff";


            document
                .querySelectorAll(
                    ".color-option"
                )
                .forEach(
                    function(option) {

                        option.classList.remove(
                            "selected"
                        );

                    }
                );


            button.classList.add(
                "selected"
            );
        }


        function saveSubject(event) {

            event.preventDefault();


            const nameInput =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectName"
                );


            const name =
                nameInput.value.trim();


            if (!name) {

                showToast(
                    "Please enter a subject name."
                );

                return;
            }


            const subjects =
                getSubjects();


            const duplicate =
                subjects.some(
                    function(subject) {

                        return (
                            subject.id !==
                                editingSubjectId &&

                            String(subject.name)
                                .trim()
                                .toLowerCase() ===
                            name.toLowerCase()
                        );

                    }
                );


            if (duplicate) {

                showToast(
                    "This subject is already added."
                );

                return;
            }


            if (editingSubjectId) {

                const subject =
                    subjects.find(
                        function(item) {

                            return (
                                item.id ===
                                editingSubjectId
                            );

                        }
                    );


                if (subject) {

                    subject.name =
                        name;

                    subject.color =
                        selectedSubjectColor;
                }


                saveSubjects(subjects);

                renderSubjects();

                closeSubjectModal();

                showToast(
                    "Subject updated successfully!"
                );

                return;
            }


            const newSubject = {

                id:
                    Date.now().toString(),

                name:
                    name,

                color:
                    selectedSubjectColor
            };


            subjects.push(
                newSubject
            );


            saveSubjects(
                subjects
            );


            renderSubjects();

            closeSubjectModal();


            showToast(
                "Subject added successfully!"
            );
        }


        function deleteSubject(id) {

            if (!id) {
                return;
            }


            const subject =
                getSubjects().find(
                    function(item) {

                        return item.id === id;

                    }
                );


            if (!subject) {
                return;
            }


            const confirmed =
                confirm(
                    `Remove "${subject.name}" from DueMate?`
                );


            if (!confirmed) {
                return;
            }


            const updated =
                getSubjects().filter(
                    function(item) {

                        return item.id !== id;

                    }
                );


            saveSubjects(updated);

            renderSubjects();


            showToast(
                "Subject removed."
            );
        }


        renderSubjects();


        /* =====================================================
           TASK DATA
           ===================================================== */

        let dashboardTasks = [];


        async function loadDashboardTasks() {

            const email =
                getCurrentUserEmail();


            if (!email) {

                dashboardTasks = [];

                renderDashboardTasks();

                updateTaskSummary(0,0,0);

                return;
            }


            try {

                const response =
                    await fetch(
                        "http://localhost:5000/api/tasks",
                        {
                            method: "GET",
                            headers: {
                                "Content-Type":
                                    "application/json",

                                "user-email":
                                    email
                            }
                        }
                    );


                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error(
                        "Unable to load tasks"
                    );
                }


                const data =
                    await response.json();


                dashboardTasks =
                    Array.isArray(data)
                        ? data
                        : (
                            Array.isArray(
                                data.tasks
                            )
                                ? data.tasks
                                : []
                        );


                renderDashboardTasks();


                updateTaskSummary(
                    dashboardTasks.length,
                    dashboardTasks.filter(
                        function(task) {

                            return isTaskCompleted(
                                task
                            );

                        }
                    ).length,
                    dashboardTasks.filter(
                        function(task) {

                            return !isTaskCompleted(
                                task
                            );

                        }
                    ).length
                );


            } catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "Dashboard task loading error:",
                    error
                );


                dashboardTasks =
                    [];


                renderDashboardTasks();


                updateTaskSummary(
                    0,
                    0,
                    0
                );
            }
        }


        function isTaskCompleted(task) {

            return (
                task.completed === true ||
                task.completed === 1 ||
                task.status === "completed" ||
                task.status === "Completed"
            );
        }


        function getTaskDate(task) {

            return (
                task.date ||
                task.task_date ||
                task.dueDate ||
                task.taskDate ||
                ""
            );
        }


        function getTaskTime(task) {

            return (
                task.time ||
                task.task_time ||
                task.taskTime ||
                ""
            );
        }


        function getTaskName(task) {

            return (
                task.name ||
                task.title ||
                task.task_name ||
                "Untitled Task"
            );
        }


        function getTaskSubject(task) {

            return (
                task.subject ||
                "No subject"
            );
        }


        function getTaskPriority(task) {

            return (
                task.priority ||
                "medium"
            );
        }


        function getTaskDescription(task) {

            return (
                task.description ||
                "No description provided."
            );
        }


        function renderDashboardTasks() {

            const todayContainer =
                document.getElementById(
                    "todayContent"
                );


            const tomorrowContainer =
                document.getElementById(
                    "tomorrowContent"
                );


            const today =
                new Date();


            const tomorrow =
                new Date(today);


            tomorrow.setDate(
                today.getDate() + 1
            );


            const todayKey =
                getDateKey(today);


            const tomorrowKey =
                getDateKey(tomorrow);


            const todayTasks =
                dashboardTasks.filter(
                    function(task) {

                        return (
                            normalizeTaskDate(
                                getTaskDate(task)
                            ) === todayKey
                        );

                    }
                );


            const tomorrowTasks =
                dashboardTasks.filter(
                    function(task) {

                        return (
                            normalizeTaskDate(
                                getTaskDate(task)
                            ) === tomorrowKey
                        );

                    }
                );


            renderTaskList(
                todayContainer,
                todayTasks,
                "ðŸ“š",
                "Nothing scheduled yet",
                "Your classes, tasks and deadlines for today will appear here."
            );


            renderTaskList(
                tomorrowContainer,
                tomorrowTasks,
                "ðŸ“",
                "Nothing scheduled yet",
                "Tomorrow's classes, tasks and deadlines will appear here."
            );
        }


        function normalizeTaskDate(value) {

            if (!value) {
                return "";
            }


            const stringValue =
                String(value);


            if (
                /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(
                    stringValue
                )
            ) {

                return stringValue;
            }


            const date =
                new Date(value);


            if (Number.isNaN(
                date.getTime()
            )) {

                return "";
            }


            return getDateKey(date);
        }


        function renderTaskList(
            container,
            tasks,
            icon,
            emptyTitle,
            emptyDescription
        ) {

            if (!container) {
                return;
            }


            container.innerHTML =
                "";


            if (!tasks.length) {

                container.innerHTML = `

                    <div class="empty-state">

                        <div class="empty-icon">
                            ${icon}
                        </div>

                        <h4>
                            ${emptyTitle}
                        </h4>

                        <p>
                            ${emptyDescription}
                        </p>

                    </div>

                `;

                return;
            }


            tasks
                .sort(
                    function(a,b) {

                        return (
                            getTaskTime(a)
                                .localeCompare(
                                    getTaskTime(b)
                                )
                        );

                    }
                )
                .forEach(
                    function(task) {

                        const item =
                            document.createElement(
                                "div"
                            );


                        item.className =
                            "content-item";


                        const time =
                            formatTaskTime(
                                getTaskTime(task)
                            );


                        item.innerHTML = `

                            <div class="content-item-icon">
                                ðŸ“
                            </div>

                            <div class="content-item-info">

                                <div class="content-item-title">
                                    ${escapeHTML(
                                        getTaskName(task)
                                    )}
                                </div>

                                <div class="content-item-meta">
                                    ${escapeHTML(
                                        getTaskSubject(task)
                                    )}
                                    ${time ? " â€¢ " + escapeHTML(time) : ""}
                                </div>

                            </div>

                            <div class="view-hint">
                                View
                            </div>

                        `;


                        item.addEventListener(
                            "click",
                            function() {

                                openTaskDetail(
                                    task
                                );

                            }
                        );


                        container.appendChild(
                            item
                        );

                    }
                );
        }


        function formatTaskTime(value) {

            if (!value) {
                return "";
            }


            const parts =
                String(value).split(":");


            if (parts.length < 2) {
                return value;
            }


            let hour =
                Number(parts[0]);


            const minute =
                parts[1];


            if (
                Number.isNaN(hour)
            ) {

                return value;
            }


            const suffix =
                hour >= 12
                    ? "PM"
                    : "AM";


            hour =
                hour % 12 || 12;


            return `${hour}:${minute} ${suffix}`;
        }


        /* =====================================================
           TASK DETAIL MODAL
           ===================================================== */

        function openTaskDetail(task) {

            const modal =
                document.getElementById(
                    "taskDetailModal"
                );


            const content =
                document.getElementById(
                    "taskDetailContent"
                );


            const taskName =
                getTaskName(task);


            const subject =
                getTaskSubject(task);


            const date =
                getTaskDate(task);


            const time =
                getTaskTime(task);


            const priority =
                getTaskPriority(task);


            const description =
                getTaskDescription(task);


            content.innerHTML = `

                <div class="detail-title">
                    ${escapeHTML(taskName)}
                </div>


                <div class="detail-row">

                    <div class="detail-row-icon">
                        ðŸ“š
                    </div>

                    <div class="detail-row-content">

                        <div class="detail-row-label">
                            Subject
                        </div>

                        <div class="detail-row-value">
                            ${escapeHTML(subject)}
                        </div>

                    </div>

                </div>


                <div class="detail-row">

                    <div class="detail-row-icon">
                        ðŸ“…
                    </div>

                    <div class="detail-row-content">

                        <div class="detail-row-label">
                            Date
                        </div>

                        <div class="detail-row-value">
                            ${escapeHTML(
                                formatReadableDate(date)
                            )}
                        </div>

                    </div>

                </div>


                <div class="detail-row">

                    <div class="detail-row-icon">
                        â°
                    </div>

                    <div class="detail-row-content">

                        <div class="detail-row-label">
                            Time
                        </div>

                        <div class="detail-row-value">
                            ${
                                time
                                    ? escapeHTML(
                                        formatTaskTime(time)
                                    )
                                    : "No time specified"
                            }
                        </div>

                    </div>

                </div>


                <div class="detail-row">

                    <div class="detail-row-icon">
                        ðŸŽ¯
                    </div>

                    <div class="detail-row-content">

                        <div class="detail-row-label">
                            Priority
                        </div>

                        <div class="detail-row-value">

                            <span class="priority-badge">
                                ${escapeHTML(priority)}
                            </span>

                        </div>

                    </div>

                </div>


                <div class="detail-row">

                    <div class="detail-row-icon">
                        ðŸ“–
                    </div>

                    <div class="detail-row-content">

                        <div class="detail-row-label">
                            Description
                        </div>

                        <div class="detail-row-value">
                            ${escapeHTML(description)}
                        </div>

                    </div>

                </div>

            `;


            modal.classList.add(
                "show"
            );
        }


        function closeTaskDetail() {

            document
                .getElementById(
                    "taskDetailModal"
                )
                .classList.remove(
                    "show"
                );
        }


        function handleTaskModalBackground(event) {

            if (
                event.target &&
                event.target.id ===
                    "taskDetailModal"
            ) {

                closeTaskDetail();
            }
        }


        function formatReadableDate(value) {

            if (!value) {
                return "No date specified";
            }


            const normalized =
                normalizeTaskDate(value);


            if (!normalized) {
                return String(value);
            }


            const parts =
                normalized.split("-");


            const date =
                new Date(
                    Number(parts[0]),
                    Number(parts[1]) - 1,
                    Number(parts[2])
                );


            return date.toLocaleDateString(
                undefined,
                {
                    weekday: "long",
                    day: "numeric",
                    month: "long",
                    year: "numeric"
                }
            );
        }


        /* =====================================================
           TASK SUMMARY
           ===================================================== */

        function updateTaskSummary(
            total,
            completed,
            pending
        ) {

            document.getElementById(
                "totalTasks"
            ).textContent =
                Number(total) || 0;


            document.getElementById(
                "completedTasks"
            ).textContent =
                Number(completed) || 0;


            document.getElementById(
                "pendingTasks"
            ).textContent =
                Number(pending) || 0;
        }


        /* =====================================================
           HTML ESCAPING
           ===================================================== */

        function escapeHTML(value) {

            const div =
                document.createElement(
                    "div"
                );


            div.textContent =
                String(value ?? "");


            return div.innerHTML;
        }


        /* =====================================================
           TOAST
           ===================================================== */

        let toastTimer;


        function showToast(message) {

            const toast =
                document.getElementById(
                    "toast"
                );


            toast.textContent =
                message;


            toast.classList.add(
                "show"
            );


            clearTimeout(
                toastTimer
            );


            toastTimer =
                setTimeout(
                    function() {

                        toast.classList.remove(
                            "show"
                        );

                    },
                    2200
                );
        }


        /* =====================================================
           ESC KEY
           ===================================================== */

        document.addEventListener(
            "keydown",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    event.key ===
                    "Escape"
                ) {

                    closeDrawer();
                    closeSubjectModal();
                    closeTaskDetail();

                }

            }
        );


        /* =====================================================
           BACKGROUND PARALLAX
           ===================================================== */

        let mouseX = 0;
        let mouseY = 0;

        let smoothX = 0;
        let smoothY = 0;


        document.addEventListener(
            "mousemove",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    window.innerWidth <=
                    700
                ) {

                    return;
                }


                mouseX =
                    event.clientX /
                    window.innerWidth -
                    0.5;


                mouseY =
                    event.clientY /
                    window.innerHeight -
                    0.5;

            }
        );


        function animateBackground() {

            smoothX +=
                (
                    mouseX -
                    smoothX
                ) * 0.025;


            smoothY +=
                (
                    mouseY -
                    smoothY
                ) * 0.025;


            const glows =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".glow"
                );


            glows.forEach(
                function(glow,index) {

                    const depth =
                        (index + 1) * 5;


                    glow.style.marginLeft =
                        `${smoothX * depth}px`;


                    glow.style.marginTop =
                        `${smoothY * depth}px`;

                }
            );


            requestAnimationFrame(
                animateBackground
            );
        }


        animateBackground();


        /* =====================================================
           LOAD DASHBOARD DATA
           ===================================================== */

        loadDashboardTasks();

    </script>

</body>

</html>

    <!-- =========================================================
         JAVASCRIPT
    ========================================================= -->

    <script>

        /* =========================================================
           BACKEND
        ========================================================= */

        const API_BASE_URL =
            "http://localhost:5000";


        /* =========================================================
           ELEMENTS
        ========================================================= */

        const forgotForm =
            document.getElementById("forgotForm");

        const emailInput =
            document.getElementById("email");

        const passwordSection =
            document.getElementById("passwordSection");

        const newPasswordInput =
            document.getElementById("newPassword");

        const confirmPasswordInput =
            document.getElementById("confirmPassword");

        const submitButton =
            document.getElementById("submitButton");

        const backLogin =
            document.getElementById("backLogin");

        const formMessage =
            document.getElementById("formMessage");

        const pageDescription =
            document.getElementById("pageDescription");


        /* =========================================================
           THEME
           Reads the theme selected elsewhere in DueMate.
        ========================================================= */

        function applySavedTheme() {

            const savedTheme =
                localStorage.getItem("duemate-theme");

            if (savedTheme === "dark") {

                document.body.classList.add("dark");

            } else {

                document.body.classList.remove("dark");

            }
        }

        applySavedTheme();


        /* =========================================================
           MESSAGE
        ========================================================= */

        function showMessage(
            text,
            type = "error"
        ) {

            formMessage.textContent = text;

            formMessage.className =
                `message show ${type}`;
        }


        function clearMessage() {

            formMessage.textContent = "";

            formMessage.className =
                "message";
        }


        /* =========================================================
           EMAIL VALIDATION
        ========================================================= */

        function isValidEmail(email) {

            return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/
                .test(email);
        }


        /* =========================================================
           PASSWORD VALIDATION
        ========================================================= */

        function isValidPassword(password) {

            return password.length >= 6;
        }


        /* =========================================================
           SET LOADING
        ========================================================= */

        function setLoading(
            loading,
            text
        ) {

            if (loading) {

                submitButton.classList.add("loading");

                submitButton.textContent = text;

            } else {

                submitButton.classList.remove("loading");

                submitButton.textContent =
                    passwordSection.classList.contains("show")
                        ? "Reset Password"
                        : "Continue";
            }
        }


        /* =========================================================
           STEP 1
           CHECK REGISTERED EMAIL
        ========================================================= */

        async function checkRegisteredEmail(email) {

            const response =
                await fetch(
                    `${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/forgot-password`,
                    {
                        method: "POST",

                        headers: {
                            "Content-Type":
                                "application/json"
                        },

                        body:
                            JSON.stringify({
                                email: email
                            })
                    }
                );

            let data = {};

            try {

                data =
                    await response.json();

            } catch (error) {

                data = {};
            }


            if (!response.ok) {

                throw new Error(
                    data.message ||
                    "This email is not registered with DueMate."
                );
            }


            return data;
        }


        /* =========================================================
           STEP 2
           RESET PASSWORD
        ========================================================= */

        async function resetPassword(
            email,
            newPassword
        ) {

            const response =
                await fetch(
                    `${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/reset-password`,
                    {
                        method: "POST",

                        headers: {
                            "Content-Type":
                                "application/json"
                        },

                        body:
                            JSON.stringify({
                                email: email,
                                newPassword: newPassword
                            })
                    }
                );


            let data = {};

            try {

                data =
                    await response.json();

            } catch (error) {

                data = {};
            }


            if (!response.ok) {

                throw new Error(
                    data.message ||
                    "Unable to reset your password."
                );
            }


            return data;
        }


        /* =========================================================
           FORM SUBMIT
        ========================================================= */

        forgotForm.addEventListener(
            "submit",
            async function (event) {

                event.preventDefault();

                clearMessage();


                const email =
                    emailInput.value
                        .trim()
                        .toLowerCase();


                /* =================================================
                   STEP 1 - CHECK EMAIL
                ================================================= */

                if (
                    !passwordSection.classList.contains("show")
                ) {

                    if (!email) {

                        showMessage(
                            "Please enter your email ID."
                        );

                        emailInput.focus();

                        return;
                    }


                    if (!isValidEmail(email)) {

                        showMessage(
                            "Please enter a valid email ID."
                        );

                        emailInput.focus();

                        return;
                    }


                    setLoading(
                        true,
                        "Checking..."
                    );


                    try {

                        const data =
                            await checkRegisteredEmail(
                                email
                            );


                        /*
                         * Save only the recovery email
                         * for this browser session.
                         */

                        sessionStorage.setItem(
                            "duemateRecoveryEmail",
                            email
                        );


                        passwordSection.classList.add(
                            "show"
                        );


                        emailInput.readOnly = true;


                        pageDescription.textContent =
                            "Create a new password for your DueMate account.";


                        submitButton.textContent =
                            "Reset Password";


                        showMessage(
                            data.message ||
                            "Account found. Please create your new password.",
                            "success"
                        );


                        newPasswordInput.focus();


                    } catch (error) {

                        console.error(
                            "Forgot password error:",
                            error
                        );


                        let errorMessage =
                            error.message ||
                            "Unable to process your request.";


                        if (
                            error instanceof TypeError
                        ) {

                            errorMessage =
                                "Unable to connect to the DueMate server. Please make sure the backend is running.";
                        }


                        showMessage(
                            errorMessage
                        );

                    } finally {

                        setLoading(
                            false
                        );
                    }


                    return;
                }


                /* =================================================
                   STEP 2 - RESET PASSWORD
                ================================================= */

                const newPassword =
                    newPasswordInput.value;

                const confirmPassword =
                    confirmPasswordInput.value;


                if (!isValidPassword(newPassword)) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Password must contain at least 6 characters."
                    );

                    newPasswordInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (
                    newPassword !==
                    confirmPassword
                ) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Passwords do not match."
                    );

                    confirmPasswordInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                setLoading(
                    true,
                    "Resetting..."
                );


                try {

                    const data =
                        await resetPassword(
                            email,
                            newPassword
                        );


                    showMessage(
                        data.message ||
                        "Password reset successfully. Redirecting to login...",
                        "success"
                    );


                    /*
                     * Remove temporary recovery data.
                     */

                    sessionStorage.removeItem(
                        "duemateRecoveryEmail"
                    );


                    /*
                     * Return to Login.
                     */

                    setTimeout(
                        function () {

                            window.location.href =
                                "index.html";

                        },
                        1200
                    );


                } catch (error) {

                    console.error(
                        "Reset password error:",
                        error
                    );


                    let errorMessage =
                        error.message ||
                        "Unable to reset your password.";


                    if (
                        error instanceof TypeError
                    ) {

                        errorMessage =
                            "Unable to connect to the DueMate server. Please make sure the backend is running.";
                    }


                    showMessage(
                        errorMessage
                    );

                } finally {

                    setLoading(
                        false
                    );
                }

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           BACK TO LOGIN
        ========================================================= */

        backLogin.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                window.location.href =
                    "index.html";
            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           INPUT CLEANUP
        ========================================================= */

        emailInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            clearMessage
        );

        newPasswordInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            clearMessage
        );

        confirmPasswordInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            clearMessage
        );


        /* =========================================================
           MOUSE PARALLAX
        ========================================================= */

        let mouseX = 0;
        let mouseY = 0;

        let smoothX = 0;
        let smoothY = 0;


        document.addEventListener(
            "mousemove",
            function (event) {

                if (
                    window.innerWidth <= 700
                ) {
                    return;
                }


                mouseX =
                    event.clientX /
                    window.innerWidth -
                    0.5;


                mouseY =
                    event.clientY /
                    window.innerHeight -
                    0.5;
            }
        );


        function animateBackground() {

            smoothX +=
                (mouseX - smoothX) *
                0.025;


            smoothY +=
                (mouseY - smoothY) *
                0.025;


            const glows =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".glow"
                );


            glows.forEach(
                function (glow, index) {

                    const depth =
                        (index + 1) * 5;


                    glow.style.marginLeft =
                        `${smoothX * depth}px`;


                    glow.style.marginTop =
                        `${smoothY * depth}px`;
                }
            );


            requestAnimationFrame(
                animateBackground
            );
        }


        animateBackground();


        /* =========================================================
           INITIAL FOCUS
        ========================================================= */

        window.addEventListener(
            "load",
            function () {

                if (
                    window.innerWidth > 650
                ) {

                    emailInput.focus();
                }
            }
        );

    </script>

</body>
</html>
