    <!-- =====================================================
         JAVASCRIPT
    ===================================================== -->

    <script>

        /* =====================================================
           THEME
           Reads saved theme only.
           Theme changing stays in Settings / registration.
        ===================================================== */

        function applySavedTheme() {

            const savedTheme =
                localStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-theme"
                ) || "light";


            if (savedTheme === "dark") {

                document.body.classList.add("dark");

            } else {

                document.body.classList.remove("dark");

            }
        }


        applySavedTheme();


        /* =====================================================
           DATE
        ===================================================== */

        function updateDate() {

            const date =
                new Date();

            document.getElementById(
                "currentDate"
            ).textContent =
                date.toLocaleDateString(
                    undefined,
                    {
                        day: "numeric",
                        month: "short",
                        year: "numeric"
                    }
                );
        }


        updateDate();


        /* =====================================================
           CREATE GROUP MODAL
        ===================================================== */

        function openCreateModal() {

            document
                .getElementById("createModal")
                .classList.add("show");

            setTimeout(() => {

                document
                    .getElementById("groupName")
                    .focus();

            },100);
        }


        function closeCreateModal() {

            document
                .getElementById("createModal")
                .classList.remove("show");

        }


        /* =====================================================
           CREATE GROUP
        ===================================================== */

        document
            .getElementById("groupForm")
            .addEventListener(
                "submit",
                function(event) {

                    event.preventDefault();


                    const name =
                        document
                            .getElementById("groupName")
                            .value
                            .trim();


                    const purpose =
                        document
                            .getElementById("groupPurpose")
                            .value
                            .trim();


                    const min =
                        Number(
                            document
                                .getElementById(
                                    "minimumMembers"
                                )
                                .value
                        );


                    const max =
                        Number(
                            document
                                .getElementById(
                                    "maximumMembers"
                                )
                                .value
                        );


                    if (max < min) {

                        showToast(
                            "Maximum members must be greater than or equal to minimum members."
                        );

                        return;
                    }


                    if (!name || !purpose) {

                        return;
                    }


                    const groupList =
                        document.getElementById(
                            "groupList"
                        );


                    const card =
                        document.createElement(
                            "article"
                        );

                    card.className =
                        "group-card";


                    card.innerHTML = `

                        <div class="group-top">

                            <div class="group-icon">
                                ðŸ‘¥
                            </div>

                            <span class="admin-badge">
                                ðŸ‘‘ ADMIN
                            </span>

                        </div>

                        <div class="group-name">
                            ${escapeHTML(name)}
                        </div>

                        <div class="group-purpose">
                            ${escapeHTML(purpose)}
                        </div>

                        <div class="group-meta">

                            <span class="meta-pill">
                                ðŸ‘¥ 1 / ${max}
                            </span>

                            <span class="meta-pill">
                                ðŸ“ 0 Tasks
                            </span>

                            <span class="meta-pill">
                                ðŸŽ® 0 Challenges
                            </span>

                        </div>

                        <div class="group-actions">

                            <button
                                class="group-action"
                                onclick="openWorkspace(
                                    '${escapeJS(name)}'
                                )">

                                Open

                            </button>

                            <button
                                class="group-action"
                                onclick="copyJoinLink(
                                    '${escapeJS(name)}-JOIN'
                                )">

                                ðŸ”— Link

                            </button>

                        </div>
                    `;


                    groupList.prepend(card);


                    const count =
                        document.getElementById(
                            "groupCount"
                        );


                    count.textContent =
                        Number(count.textContent) + 1;


                    closeCreateModal();


                    document
                        .getElementById("groupForm")
                        .reset();


                    showToast(
                        "ðŸŽ‰ Group created successfully!"
                    );

                }
            );


        /* =====================================================
           WORKSPACE
        ===================================================== */

        function openWorkspace(groupName) {

            document.getElementById(
                "workspaceGroupName"
            ).textContent =
                groupName;


            document.getElementById(
                "workspaceSubtitle"
            ).textContent =
                "Currently viewing this group workspace.";


            document
                .getElementById("workspacePanel")
                .scrollIntoView({
                    behavior: "smooth",
                    block: "start"
                });


            showToast(
                `Opened ${groupName}`
            );
        }


        /* =====================================================
           COPY JOIN LINK
        ===================================================== */

        function copyJoinLink(code) {

            const link =
                `${window.location.origin}/join.html?code=${encodeURIComponent(code)}`;


            if (
                navigator.clipboard &&
                navigator.clipboard.writeText
            ) {

                navigator.clipboard
                    .writeText(link)
                    .then(() => {

                        showToast(
                            "ðŸ”— Join link copied!"
                        );

                    })
                    .catch(() => {

                        showToast(
                            "Join link generated: " + link
                        );

                    });

            } else {

                showToast(
                    "Join link generated: " + link
                );
            }
        }


        /* =====================================================
           REQUESTS
        ===================================================== */

        function acceptRequest(button) {

            const card =
                button.closest(
                    ".request-card"
                );


            if (card) {

                card.remove();

                checkRequests();

                showToast(
                    "âœ… Join request accepted."
                );
            }
        }


        function rejectRequest(button) {

            const card =
                button.closest(
                    ".request-card"
                );


            if (card) {

                card.remove();

                checkRequests();

                showToast(
                    "Request rejected."
                );
            }
        }


        function checkRequests() {

            const cards =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    "#requestList .request-card"
                );


            if (cards.length === 0) {

                document.getElementById(
                    "noRequests"
                ).style.display =
                    "block";
            }
        }


        /* =====================================================
           CHALLENGE MODAL
        ===================================================== */

        function openChallengeModal() {

            document
                .getElementById("challengeModal")
                .classList.add("show");


            const today =
                new Date();

            today.setDate(
                today.getDate() + 7
            );


            document.getElementById(
                "challengeEnd"
            ).value =
                today.toISOString()
                    .split("T")[0];
        }


        function closeChallengeModal() {

            document
                .getElementById("challengeModal")
                .classList.remove("show");

        }


        /* =====================================================
           CREATE CHALLENGE
        ===================================================== */

        document
            .getElementById("challengeForm")
            .addEventListener(
                "submit",
                function(event) {

                    event.preventDefault();


                    const title =
                        document
                            .getElementById(
                                "challengeTitle"
                            )
                            .value
                            .trim();


                    const subject =
                        document
                            .getElementById(
                                "challengeSubject"
                            )
                            .value
                            .trim();


                    const points =
                        document
                            .getElementById(
                                "challengePoints"
                            )
                            .value;


                    if (!title || !subject) {

                        return;
                    }


                    const list =
                        document.querySelector(
                            ".challenge-list"
                        );


                    const card =
                        document.createElement(
                            "article"
                        );


                    card.className =
                        "challenge-card";


                    card.innerHTML = `

                        <div class="challenge-icon">
                            ðŸŽ¯
                        </div>

                        <h4>
                            ${escapeHTML(title)}
                        </h4>

                        <p>
                            ${escapeHTML(subject)}
                            challenge created by the
                            group admin.
                        </p>

                        <span class="points">
                            +${escapeHTML(points)}
                            points
                        </span>

                    `;


                    list.appendChild(card);


                    closeChallengeModal();


                    document
                        .getElementById("challengeForm")
                        .reset();


                    showToast(
                        "ðŸŽ® Challenge created!"
                    );

                }
            );


        /* =====================================================
           SCROLL TO GAMES
        ===================================================== */

        function scrollToGames() {

            document
                .getElementById("gamesSection")
                .scrollIntoView({
                    behavior: "smooth",
                    block: "start"
                });
        }


        /* =====================================================
           TOAST
        ===================================================== */

        let toastTimer;


        function showToast(message) {

            const toast =
                document.getElementById(
                    "toast"
                );


            toast.textContent =
                message;


            toast.classList.add("show");


            clearTimeout(toastTimer);


            toastTimer =
                setTimeout(() => {

                    toast.classList.remove(
                        "show"
                    );

                },3000);
        }


        /* =====================================================
           ESCAPE HTML
        ===================================================== */

        function escapeHTML(value) {

            const div =
                document.createElement("div");

            div.textContent =
                value;

            return div.innerHTML;
        }


        function escapeJS(value) {

            return String(value)
                .replace(/\\/g,"\\\\")
                .replace(/'/g,"\\'")
                .replace(/"/g,'\\"')
                .replace(/\n/g,"\\n")
                .replace(/\r/g,"\\r");
        }


        /* =====================================================
           CLOSE MODALS OUTSIDE CLICK
        ===================================================== */

        document
            .getElementById("createModal")
            .addEventListener(
                "click",
                function(event) {

                    if (
                        event.target === this
                    ) {

                        closeCreateModal();

                    }
                }
            );


        document
            .getElementById("challengeModal")
            .addEventListener(
                "click",
                function(event) {

                    if (
                        event.target === this
                    ) {

                        closeChallengeModal();

                    }
                }
            );


        /* =====================================================
           ESC KEY
        ===================================================== */

        document.addEventListener(
            "keydown",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    event.key === "Escape"
                ) {

                    closeCreateModal();

                    closeChallengeModal();

                }
            }
        );


        /* =====================================================
           NAVIGATION
        ===================================================== */

        function goDashboard() {

            window.location.href =
                "dashboard.html";
        }


        function goNotifications() {

            window.location.href =
                "notifications.html";
        }


        /* =====================================================
           BACKGROUND PARALLAX
        ===================================================== */

        let mouseX = 0;
        let mouseY = 0;

        let smoothX = 0;
        let smoothY = 0;


        document.addEventListener(
            "mousemove",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    window.innerWidth <= 700
                ) {
                    return;
                }


                mouseX =
                    event.clientX /
                    window.innerWidth -
                    .5;


                mouseY =
                    event.clientY /
                    window.innerHeight -
                    .5;

            }
        );


        function animateBackground() {

            smoothX +=
                (mouseX - smoothX) *
                .025;


            smoothY +=
                (mouseY - smoothY) *
                .025;


            const glows =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".glow"
                );


            glows.forEach(
                (glow,index) => {

                    const depth =
                        (index + 1) * 5;


                    glow.style.marginLeft =
                        `${smoothX * depth}px`;


                    glow.style.marginTop =
                        `${smoothY * depth}px`;

                }
            );


            requestAnimationFrame(
                animateBackground
            );
        }


        animateBackground();

    </script>

</body>
</html>

<script>

    /* =========================================================
       CONSTANTS
    ========================================================= */

    const API_BASE =
        "http://localhost:5000";


    /* =========================================================
       ELEMENTS
    ========================================================= */

    const loginForm =
        document.getElementById("loginForm");

    const emailInput =
        document.getElementById("email");

    const passwordInput =
        document.getElementById("password");

    const passwordToggle =
        document.getElementById("passwordToggle");

    const loginButton =
        document.getElementById("loginButton");

    const message =
        document.getElementById("message");

    const rememberMe =
        document.getElementById("rememberMe");

    const registerButton =
        document.getElementById("registerButton");

    const forgotButton =
        document.getElementById("forgotButton");


    /* =========================================================
       EMAIL
    ========================================================= */

    function normalizeEmail(value) {

        return value
            .trim()
            .toLowerCase();

    }


    /* =========================================================
       SHOW MESSAGE
    ========================================================= */

    function showMessage(text, type = "error") {

        message.textContent = text;

        message.className =
            "message show " + type;

    }


    function clearMessage() {

        message.textContent = "";

        message.className =
            "message";

    }


    /* =========================================================
       PASSWORD TOGGLE
    ========================================================= */

    passwordToggle.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            if (
                passwordInput.type === "password"
            ) {

                passwordInput.type = "text";

                passwordToggle.textContent = "ðŸ™ˆ";

            } else {

                passwordInput.type = "password";

                passwordToggle.textContent = "ðŸ‘ï¸";

            }

        }
    );


    /* =========================================================
       THEME
    ========================================================= */

    function getUserTheme(email) {

        const key =
            "duemate-theme-" +
            normalizeEmail(email);

        const userTheme =
            localStorage.getItem(key);

        if (
            userTheme === "dark" ||
            userTheme === "light"
        ) {

            return userTheme;

        }


        const oldTheme =
            localStorage.getItem("duemate-theme");

        if (
            oldTheme === "dark" ||
            oldTheme === "light"
        ) {

            return oldTheme;

        }


        return "light";

    }


    function applyTheme(theme) {

        if (theme === "dark") {

            document.body.classList.add("dark");

        } else {

            document.body.classList.remove("dark");

        }

    }


    applyTheme(
        localStorage.getItem("duemate-theme") === "dark"
            ? "dark"
            : "light"
    );


    emailInput.addEventListener(
        "blur",
        function () {

            const email =
                normalizeEmail(
                    emailInput.value
                );

            if (!email) return;

            applyTheme(
                getUserTheme(email)
            );

        }
    );


    /* =========================================================
       LOGIN
    ========================================================= */

    loginForm.addEventListener(
        "submit",
        async function (event) {

            event.preventDefault();

            clearMessage();


            const email =
                normalizeEmail(
                    emailInput.value
                );

            const password =
                passwordInput.value;


            if (!email) {

                showMessage(
                    "Please enter your email ID."
                );

                emailInput.focus();

                return;

            }


            if (
                !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/
                    .test(email)
            ) {

                showMessage(
                    "Please enter a valid email ID."
                );

                emailInput.focus();

                return;

            }


            if (!password) {

                showMessage(
                    "Please enter your password."
                );

                passwordInput.focus();

                return;

            }


            loginButton.disabled = true;

            loginButton.textContent =
                "Signing in...";


            try {

                console.log(
                    "Sending login request for:",
                    email
                );


                const response =
                    await fetch(
                        API_BASE +
                        "/api/auth/login",
                        {
                            method: "POST",

                            headers: {
                                "Content-Type":
                                    "application/json"
                            },

                            body:
                                JSON.stringify({
                                    email: email,
                                    password: password
                                })
                        }
                    );


                let data = {};

                try {

                    data =
                        await response.json();

                } catch {

                    data = {};

                }


                console.log(
                    "Login response:",
                    data
                );


                if (!response.ok) {

                    throw new Error(
                        data.message ||
                        "Invalid email or password."
                    );

                }


                /*
                   IMPORTANT:

                   Backend must return:

                   {
                       user: {
                           id: ...,
                           email: ...,
                           name: ...,
                           theme: ...
                       }
                   }
                */

                if (!data.user) {

                    throw new Error(
                        "Login succeeded, but the server did not return user information."
                    );

                }


                /* =================================================
                   USER EMAIL
                ================================================= */

                const userEmail =
                    normalizeEmail(
                        data.user.email ||
                        email
                    );


                /* =================================================
                   THEME
                ================================================= */

                let theme =
                    getUserTheme(userEmail);


                if (
                    data.user.theme === "dark" ||
                    data.user.theme === "light"
                ) {

                    theme =
                        data.user.theme;

                    localStorage.setItem(
                        "duemate-theme-" +
                        userEmail,
                        theme
                    );

                }


                applyTheme(theme);


                /* =================================================
                   CREATE COMPLETE USER SESSION
                ================================================= */

                const loggedInUser = {

                    ...data.user,

                    email:
                        userEmail,

                    theme:
                        theme

                };


                /*
                   THIS IS THE MOST IMPORTANT PART.

                   Dashboard must read EXACTLY:

                   sessionStorage.getItem("duemate-user")
                */

                sessionStorage.setItem(
                    "duemate-user",
                    JSON.stringify(
                        loggedInUser
                    )
                );


                /*
                   Extra compatibility key.
                */

                sessionStorage.setItem(
                    "dueMateUser",
                    JSON.stringify(
                        loggedInUser
                    )
                );


                /*
                   Store theme for current tab.
                */

                sessionStorage.setItem(
                    "duemate-theme",
                    theme
                );


                /*
                   Remember email only.
                   NEVER store password.
                */

                if (
                    rememberMe.checked
                ) {

                    localStorage.setItem(
                        "duemate-remembered-email",
                        userEmail
                    );

                } else {

                    localStorage.removeItem(
                        "duemate-remembered-email"
                    );

                }


                /* =================================================
                   SUCCESS
                ================================================= */

                showMessage(
                    "Login successful! Opening DueMate...",
                    "success"
                );


                /*
                   Small delay so the user sees success.
                */

                setTimeout(
                    function () {

                        window.location.replace(
                            "dashboard.html"
                        );

                    },
                    400
                );

            }

            catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "LOGIN ERROR:",
                    error
                );


                if (
                    error instanceof TypeError
                ) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Cannot connect to DueMate server. Please start the backend using node server.js."
                    );

                } else {

                    showMessage(
                        error.message ||
                        "Unable to login."
                    );

                }

            }

            finally {

                loginButton.disabled =
                    false;

                loginButton.textContent =
                    "Login";

            }

        }
    );


    /* =========================================================
       REGISTER
    ========================================================= */

    registerButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            window.location.href =
                "register.html";

        }
    );


    /* =========================================================
       FORGOT PASSWORD
    ========================================================= */

    forgotButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function () {

            window.location.href =
                "forgot-password.html";

        }
    );


    /* =========================================================
       REMEMBERED EMAIL
    ========================================================= */

    window.addEventListener(
        "DOMContentLoaded",
        function () {

            const rememberedEmail =
                localStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-remembered-email"
                );


            if (rememberedEmail) {

                emailInput.value =
                    rememberedEmail;

                rememberMe.checked =
                    true;

                applyTheme(
                    getUserTheme(
                        rememberedEmail
                    )
                );

            }

        }
    );

</script>

</body>
</html>

===== C:\DueMate\frontend\notifications.html =====

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

    <title>DueMate - Notifications</title>

    <style>

        /* =====================================================
           DUE MATE - NOTIFICATIONS
           Integrated HTML + CSS + JavaScript
           Step 6
        ===================================================== */

        * {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        :root {
            --bg: #f5f6ff;
            --surface: rgba(255,255,255,0.72);
            --surface-solid: #ffffff;

            --text: #25263a;
            --muted: #777b91;

            --border: rgba(255,255,255,0.9);

            --purple: #756cff;
            --blue: #5d9cff;
            --pink: #ff86b6;
            --mint: #55d5b0;
            --yellow: #ffd76b;
            --orange: #ffad66;
            --red: #ff6f91;

            --grid: rgba(91,99,150,0.055);
            --shadow: rgba(60,66,120,0.14);
        }


        /* =====================================================
           DARK THEME
        ===================================================== */

                ðŸ“…

                ðŸ””

                </span>

                    created.
                -->

            </div>

            <div
                class="empty-state"
                id="emptyState">

                <div class="empty-icon">
                    ðŸ””
                </div>

                <h3>
                    You're all caught up!
                </h3>

                <p>
                    There are no notifications to show
                    right now. New task reminders,
                    timetable updates, group activity
                    and challenge notifications will
                    appear here.
                </p>

            </div>

        </section>


        <!-- =================================================
             NOTIFICATION SETTINGS
        ================================================= -->

        <section class="settings-card">

            <h3>
                ðŸ”” Notification Preferences
            </h3>

            <p>
                Control how DueMate alerts you.
                Detailed notification settings are also
                available in Profile & Settings.
            </p>


            <div class="setting-row">

                        Receive DueMate notifications.

    <!-- =====================================================
         CLEAR ALL MODAL
    ===================================================== -->

    <div
        class="modal-overlay"
        id="clearModal">

        <div class="modal">

            <h2>
                Clear notifications?
            </h2>

            <p>
                This will remove all notifications from
                this page. This action cannot be undone.
            </p>


            <div class="modal-actions">

                <button
                    class="modal-btn cancel"
                    type="button"
                    onclick="closeClearModal()">

                    Cancel

                    onclick="clearAllNotifications()">

                    Clear All

                </button>

            </div>

    <script>

        /* =====================================================
           DUE MATE - NOTIFICATIONS JAVASCRIPT
        ===================================================== */


        /* =====================================================
           STATE
        ===================================================== */

        let notifications = [];

        let currentFilter = "all";


        /* =====================================================
           DATE
        ===================================================== */

        function updateCurrentDate() {

            const dateElement =
                document.getElementById(
                    "currentDate"
                );

            const today =
                new Date();

            dateElement.textContent =
                today.toLocaleDateString(
                    undefined,
                    {
                        day: "numeric",
                        month: "short",
                        year: "numeric"
                    }
                );
        }


        /* =====================================================
           RENDER NOTIFICATIONS
        ===================================================== */

        function renderNotifications() {

            const list =
                document.getElementById(
                    "notificationList"
                );

            const empty =
                document.getElementById(
                    "emptyState"
                );

            list.innerHTML = "";


            const filtered =
                notifications.filter(
                    notification => {

                        if (
                            currentFilter ===
                            "all"
                        ) {

                            return true;
                        }


                        if (
                            currentFilter ===
                            "unread"
                        ) {

                            return !notification.read;
                        }


                        return (
                            notification.category ===
                            currentFilter
                        );
                    }
                );


            if (filtered.length === 0) {

                empty.style.display =
                    "block";

            } else {

                empty.style.display =
                    "none";
            }


            filtered.forEach(
                notification => {

                    const item =
                        document.createElement(
                            "article"
                        );

                    item.className =
                        "notification-item" +
                        (
                            notification.read
                                ? ""
                                : " unread"
                        );


                    const icon =
                        document.createElement(
                            "div"
                        );

                    icon.className =
                        "notification-icon " +
                        getIconClass(
                            notification.category
                        );

                    icon.textContent =
                        notification.icon ||
                        "ðŸ””";


                    const content =
                        document.createElement(
                            "div"
                        );

                    content.className =
                        "notification-content";


                    const title =
                        document.createElement(
                            "div"
                        );

                    title.className =
                        "notification-title";


                    const titleText =
                        document.createElement(
                            "strong"
                        );

                    titleText.textContent =
                        notification.title;


                    title.appendChild(
                        titleText
                    );


                    if (!notification.read) {

                        const dot =
                            document.createElement(
                                "span"
                            );

                        dot.className =
                            "unread-dot";

                        title.appendChild(
                            dot
                        );
                    }


                    const message =
                        document.createElement(
                            "div"
                        );

                    message.className =
                        "notification-message";

                    message.textContent =
                        notification.message;


                    const time =
                        document.createElement(
                            "div"
                        );

                    time.className =
                        "notification-time";

                    time.textContent =
                        notification.time ||
                        "";


                    content.appendChild(
                        title
                    );

                    content.appendChild(
                        message
                    );

                    content.appendChild(
                        time
                    );


                    const actions =
                        document.createElement(
                            "div"
                        );

                    actions.className =
                        "notification-actions";


                    const readButton =
                        document.createElement(
                            "button"
                        );

                    readButton.type =
                        "button";

                    readButton.className =
                        "item-action";

                    readButton.title =
                        notification.read
                            ? "Mark as unread"
                            : "Mark as read";

                    readButton.textContent =
                        notification.read
                            ? "â†©"
                            : "âœ“";


                    readButton.addEventListener(
                        "click",
                        function() {

                            toggleRead(
                                notification.id
                            );

                        }
                    );


                    const deleteButton =
                        document.createElement(
                            "button"
                        );

                    deleteButton.type =
                        "button";

                    deleteButton.className =
                        "item-action delete";

                    deleteButton.title =
                        "Delete";

                    deleteButton.textContent =
                        "ðŸ—‘";


                    deleteButton.addEventListener(
                        "click",
                        function() {

                            deleteNotification(
                                notification.id
                            );

                        }
                    );


                    actions.appendChild(
                        readButton
                    );

                    actions.appendChild(
                        deleteButton
                    );


                    item.appendChild(
                        icon
                    );

                    item.appendChild(
                        content
                    );

                    item.appendChild(
                        actions
                    );


                    list.appendChild(
                        item
                    );

                }
            );


            updateUnreadCount();
        }


        /* =====================================================
           ICON CLASS
        ===================================================== */

        function getIconClass(category) {

            if (
                category === "tasks"
            ) {
                return "task";
            }

            if (
                category === "groups"
            ) {
                return "group";
            }

            if (
                category === "timetable"
            ) {
                return "timetable";
            }

            if (
                category === "challenges"
            ) {
                return "challenge";
            }

            if (
                category === "deadline"
            ) {
                return "deadline";
            }

            return "";
        }


        /* =====================================================
           READ / UNREAD
        ===================================================== */

        function toggleRead(id) {

            const item =
                notifications.find(
                    notification =>
                        notification.id === id
                );


            if (!item) {
                return;
            }


            item.read =
                !item.read;


            renderNotifications();
        }


        function markAllRead() {

            notifications.forEach(
                notification => {

                    notification.read =
                        true;

                }
            );


            renderNotifications();
        }


        /* =====================================================
           DELETE
        ===================================================== */

        function deleteNotification(id) {

            notifications =
                notifications.filter(
                    notification =>
                        notification.id !== id
                );


            renderNotifications();
        }


        /* =====================================================
           FILTER
        ===================================================== */

        function setFilter(
            filter,
            button
        ) {

            currentFilter =
                filter;


            document
                .querySelectorAll(
                    ".filter-btn"
                )
                .forEach(
                    item => {

                        item.classList.remove(
                            "active"
                        );

                    }
                );


            button.classList.add(
                "active"
            );


            renderNotifications();
        }


        /* =====================================================
           UNREAD COUNT
        ===================================================== */

        function updateUnreadCount() {

            const unread =
                notifications.filter(
                    notification =>
                        !notification.read
                ).length;


            document.getElementById(
                "unreadCount"
            ).textContent =
                unread;


            const dot =
                document.getElementById(
                    "notificationDot"
                );


            dot.style.display =
                unread > 0
                    ? "block"
                    : "none";
        }


        /* =====================================================
           CLEAR ALL
        ===================================================== */

        function openClearModal() {

            document
                .getElementById(
                    "clearModal"
                )
                .classList.add(
                    "show"
                );
        }


        function closeClearModal() {

            document
                .getElementById(
                    "clearModal"
                )
                .classList.remove(
                    "show"
                );
        }


        function clearAllNotifications() {

            notifications = [];

            renderNotifications();

            closeClearModal();
        }


        /* =====================================================
           NOTIFICATION SETTING
        ===================================================== */

        function handleNotificationToggle() {

            const enabled =
                document.getElementById(
                    "notificationToggle"
                ).checked;


            localStorage.setItem(
                "duemate-notifications",
                enabled
                    ? "on"
                    : "off"
            );
        }


        function loadNotificationSetting() {

            const saved =
                localStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-notifications"
                );


            if (saved === "off") {

                document.getElementById(
                    "notificationToggle"
                ).checked = false;
            }
        }


        /* =====================================================
           SOUND SETTING
        ===================================================== */

        function saveSoundSetting() {

            const enabled =
                document.getElementById(
                    "soundToggle"
                ).checked;


            localStorage.setItem(
                "duemate-notification-sound",
                enabled
                    ? "on"
                    : "off"
            );
        }


        function loadSoundSetting() {

            const saved =
                localStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-notification-sound"
                );


            if (saved === "off") {

                document.getElementById(
                    "soundToggle"
                ).checked = false;
            }
        }


        /* =====================================================
           THEME
           
           IMPORTANT:
           This page DOES NOT provide theme changing.

           It only reads the theme selected through
           registration / Settings.
        ===================================================== */

        function applySavedTheme() {

            const savedTheme =
                localStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-theme"
                ) || "system";


            if (
                savedTheme === "dark"
            ) {

                document.body.classList.add(
                    "dark"
                );

                return;
            }


            if (
                savedTheme === "light"
            ) {

                document.body.classList.remove(
                    "dark"
                );

                return;
            }


            const systemDark =
                window.matchMedia(
                    "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
                ).matches;


            document.body.classList.toggle(
                "dark",
                systemDark
            );
        }


        applySavedTheme();


        /* =====================================================
           SYSTEM THEME LISTENER
        ===================================================== */

        window
            .matchMedia(
                "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
            )
            .addEventListener(
                "change",
                function() {

                    const savedTheme =
                        localStorage.getItem(
                            "duemate-theme"
                        ) || "system";


                    if (
                        savedTheme ===
                        "system"
                    ) {

                        applySavedTheme();
                    }

                }
            );


        /* =====================================================
           MOUSE PARALLAX
        ===================================================== */

        let mouseX = 0;
        let mouseY = 0;

        let smoothX = 0;
        let smoothY = 0;


        document.addEventListener(
            "mousemove",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    window.innerWidth <= 700
                ) {
                    return;
                }


                mouseX =
                    event.clientX /
                    window.innerWidth -
                    0.5;


                mouseY =
                    event.clientY /
                    window.innerHeight -
                    0.5;

            }
        );


        function animateBackground() {

            smoothX +=
                (mouseX - smoothX) *
                0.025;


            smoothY +=
                (mouseY - smoothY) *
                0.025;


            const glows =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".glow"
                );


            glows.forEach(
                (glow,index) => {

                    const depth =
                        (index + 1) * 5;


                    glow.style.marginLeft =
                        `${smoothX * depth}px`;


                    glow.style.marginTop =
                        `${smoothY * depth}px`;

                }
            );


            requestAnimationFrame(
                animateBackground
            );
        }


        animateBackground();


        /* =====================================================
           MODAL OUTSIDE CLICK
        ===================================================== */

        document
            .getElementById(
                "clearModal"
            )
            .addEventListener(
                "click",
                function(event) {

                    if (
                        event.target === this
                    ) {

                        closeClearModal();
                    }

                }
            );


        /* =====================================================
           ESCAPE KEY
        ===================================================== */

        document.addEventListener(
            "keydown",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    event.key === "Escape"
                ) {

                    closeClearModal();
                }

            }
        );


        /* =====================================================
           NAVIGATION
        ===================================================== */

        function goDashboard() {

            window.location.href =
                "dashboard.html";
        }


        function goCalendar() {

            window.location.href =
                "calendar.html";
        }


        /* =====================================================
           INITIALIZE
        ===================================================== */

        updateCurrentDate();

        loadNotificationSetting();

        loadSoundSetting();

        renderNotifications();

    </script>

</body>
</html>

    <style>

        /* =========================================================

           Notifications = settings.html

        :root {

                rgba(20, 26, 49, 0.80);

            --surface-solid:
                #151b32;

            --surface-soft:
                rgba(28, 35, 62, 0.60);

            --text:
                #f4f5ff;

            --muted:
                #aeb3ca;

            --border:
                rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.09);

            --purple:
                #958cff;

            --blue:
                #73adff;

            --pink:
                #ff91bf;

            --mint:
                #64dfc0;

            --yellow:
                #ffe08a;

            --grid:
                rgba(185, 195, 255, 0.035);

            --shadow:
                rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
        }


        body {

            min-height: 100vh;

            font-family:
                "Segoe UI",
                Inter,
                Arial,
                sans-serif;

            color:
                var(--text);

            background:
                var(--bg);

            overflow-x: hidden;

            transition:
                background 0.4s ease,
                color 0.4s ease;
        }


        button,
        input {
            font: inherit;
        }


        button {
            cursor: pointer;
        }


        /* =========================================================
           BACKGROUND
        ========================================================= */


        .background {

            position: fixed;

            inset: 0;

            z-index: -10;

            overflow: hidden;

            background:
                var(--bg);

            transition:
                background 0.4s ease;
        }


        .background::before {

            content: "";

            position: absolute;

            inset: 0;

            background:

                radial-gradient(
                    circle at 15% 15%,
                    rgba(117, 108, 255, 0.12),
                    transparent 30%
                ),

                radial-gradient(
                    circle at 85% 30%,
                    rgba(85, 213, 176, 0.10),
                    transparent 28%
                ),

                radial-gradient(
                    circle at 50% 90%,
                    rgba(255, 134, 182, 0.10),
                    transparent 30%
                );
        }


        .grid {

            position: absolute;

            inset: -100px;

            background-image:

                linear-gradient(
                    var(--grid) 1px,
                    transparent 1px
                ),

                linear-gradient(
                    90deg,
                    var(--grid) 1px,
                    transparent 1px
                );

            background-size:
                44px 44px;

            transform:
                perspective(500px)
                rotateX(55deg)
                scale(1.4);

            transform-origin:
                center bottom;

            mask-image:
                linear-gradient(
                    to bottom,
                    transparent 5%,
                    black 35%,
                    black 75%,
                    transparent 100%
                );

            animation:
                gridMove 30s linear infinite;
        }


        @keyframes gridMove {

            from {

                background-position:
                    0 0,
                    0 0;
            }

            to {

                background-position:
                    44px 44px,
                    44px 44px;
            }
        }


        .glow {

            position: absolute;

            border-radius: 50%;

            filter:
                blur(90px);

            pointer-events:
                none;

            opacity:
                0.25;
        }


        .glow-one {

            width: 430px;
            height: 430px;

            left: -180px;
            top: -160px;

            background:
                var(--purple);

            animation:
                glowOne 18s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
        }


        .glow-two {

            width: 400px;
            height: 400px;

            right: -170px;
            top: 30%;

            background:
                var(--mint);

            animation:
                glowTwo 22s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
        }


        .glow-three {

            width: 380px;
            height: 380px;

            left: 35%;
            bottom: -220px;

            background:
                var(--pink);

            animation:
                glowThree 25s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
        }
