    <!-- =========================================================
         JAVASCRIPT
    ========================================================= -->

    <script>


        /* =========================================================
           ELEMENTS
        ========================================================= */


        const dashboardBtn =
            document.getElementById(
                "dashboardBtn"
            );


        const settingsBtn =
            document.getElementById(
                "settingsBtn"
            );


        const settingsShortcut =
            document.getElementById(
                "settingsShortcut"
            );


        const editProfileBtn =
            document.getElementById(
                "editProfileBtn"
            );


        const changePasswordBtn =
            document.getElementById(
                "changePasswordBtn"
            );


        const logoutBtn =
            document.getElementById(
                "logoutBtn"
            );


        const modalOverlay =
            document.getElementById(
                "modalOverlay"
            );


        const closeModalBtn =
            document.getElementById(
                "closeModalBtn"
            );


        const cancelEditBtn =
            document.getElementById(
                "cancelEditBtn"
            );


        const saveProfileBtn =
            document.getElementById(
                "saveProfileBtn"
            );


        const toast =
            document.getElementById(
                "toast"
            );


        /* =========================================================
           PROFILE ELEMENTS
        ========================================================= */


        const profileName =
            document.getElementById(
                "profileName"
            );


        const profileEmail =
            document.getElementById(
                "profileEmail"
            );


        const fullName =
            document.getElementById(
                "fullName"
            );


        const emailAddress =
            document.getElementById(
                "emailAddress"
            );


        const mobileNumber =
            document.getElementById(
                "mobileNumber"
            );


        const studentId =
            document.getElementById(
                "studentId"
            );


        const collegeName =
            document.getElementById(
                "collegeName"
            );


        const department =
            document.getElementById(
                "department"
            );


        const course =
            document.getElementById(
                "course"
            );


        const semester =
            document.getElementById(
                "semester"
            );


        const avatarLetter =
            document.getElementById(
                "avatarLetter"
            );


        /* =========================================================
           EDIT ELEMENTS
        ========================================================= */


        const editName =
            document.getElementById(
                "editName"
            );


        const editMobile =
            document.getElementById(
                "editMobile"
            );


        const editCollege =
            document.getElementById(
                "editCollege"
            );


        const editDepartment =
            document.getElementById(
                "editDepartment"
            );


        const editCourse =
            document.getElementById(
                "editCourse"
            );


        const editSemester =
            document.getElementById(
                "editSemester"
            );


        /* =========================================================
           GET LOGGED-IN USER
        ========================================================= */


        function getLoggedInUser() {

            const userData =
                sessionStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-user"
                );


            if (!userData) {

                return null;
            }


            try {

                return JSON.parse(
                    userData
                );

            } catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "Unable to read logged-in user:",
                    error
                );

                return null;
            }
        }


        /* =========================================================
           REDIRECT IF NOT LOGGED IN
        ========================================================= */


        const currentUser =
            getLoggedInUser();


        if (!currentUser) {

            window.location.replace(
                "index.html?redirect=profile"
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           SAFE VALUE
        ========================================================= */


        function getValue(
            object,
            keys,
            fallback = "Not available"
        ) {

            for (
                const key of keys
            ) {

                if (
                    object &&
                    object[key] !== undefined &&
                    object[key] !== null &&
                    String(
                        object[key]
                    ).trim() !== ""
                ) {

                    return String(
                        object[key]
                    ).trim();
                }
            }


            return fallback;
        }


        /* =========================================================
           INITIAL PROFILE DATA
        ========================================================= */


        function getProfileData() {

            const user =
                getLoggedInUser();


            if (!user) {

                return null;
            }


            return {

                name: getValue(
                    user,
                    [
                        "name",
                        "fullName",
                        "fullname",
                        "username"
                    ],
                    "Student"
                ),


                email: getValue(
                    user,
                    [
                        "email",
                        "emailId",
                        "emailID"
                    ],
                    "Not available"
                ),


                mobile: getValue(
                    user,
                    [
                        "mobile",
                        "mobileNumber",
                        "phone",
                        "phoneNumber"
                    ],
                    "Not available"
                ),


                studentId: getValue(
                    user,
                    [
                        "studentId",
                        "studentID",
                        "rollNumber",
                        "registerNumber"
                    ],
                    "Not available"
                ),


                college: getValue(
                    user,
                    [
                        "college",
                        "collegeName",
                        "institution"
                    ],
                    "Not available"
                ),


                /*
                    Department has a default example.
                    It will appear dim when no actual
                    department is saved.
                */

                department: getValue(
                    user,
                    [
                        "department",
                        "dept"
                    ],
                    "Information Technology"
                ),


                /*
                    Course has a default example.
                    It will appear dim when no actual
                    course is saved.
                */

                course: getValue(
                    user,
                    [
                        "course",
                        "program",
                        "degree"
                    ],
                    "B.Tech"
                ),


                semester: getValue(
                    user,
                    [
                        "semester",
                        "yearSemester",
                        "year"
                    ],
                    "Not available"
                )

            };
        }


        /* =========================================================
           FIRST LETTER
        ========================================================= */


        function getInitial(name) {

            if (
                !name ||
                name === "Student"
            ) {

                return "U";
            }


            return name
                .trim()
                .charAt(0)
                .toUpperCase();
        }


        /* =========================================================
           DISPLAY PROFILE
        ========================================================= */


        function displayProfile() {

            const data =
                getProfileData();


            if (!data) {

                return;
            }


            profileName.textContent =
                data.name;


            profileEmail.textContent =
                data.email;


            fullName.textContent =
                data.name;


            emailAddress.textContent =
                data.email;


            mobileNumber.textContent =
                data.mobile;


            studentId.textContent =
                data.studentId;


            collegeName.textContent =
                data.college;


            department.textContent =
                data.department;


            course.textContent =
                data.course;


            semester.textContent =
                data.semester;


            avatarLetter.textContent =
                getInitial(
                    data.name
                );


            /*
                Apply dim style only when
                Department is using the default
                example value.
            */

            if (
                data.department ===
                "Information Technology"
            ) {

                department.classList.add(
                    "example-value"
                );

            } else {

                department.classList.remove(
                    "example-value"
                );
            }


            /*
                Apply dim style only when
                Course is using the default
                example value.
            */

            if (
                data.course ===
                "B.Tech"
            ) {

                course.classList.add(
                    "example-value"
                );

            } else {

                course.classList.remove(
                    "example-value"
                );
            }

        }


        /* =========================================================
           APPLY SAVED THEME

           Theme selection belongs to settings.html.
        ========================================================= */


        function applySavedTheme() {

            const savedTheme =
                localStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-theme"
                );


            if (
                savedTheme === "dark"
            ) {

                document.body.classList.add(
                    "dark"
                );

            } else {

                document.body.classList.remove(
                    "dark"
                );
            }
        }


        /* =========================================================
           TOAST
        ========================================================= */


        let toastTimer;


        function showToast(message) {

            clearTimeout(
                toastTimer
            );


            toast.textContent =
                message;


            toast.classList.add(
                "show"
            );


            toastTimer =
                setTimeout(
                    function () {

                        toast.classList.remove(
                            "show"
                        );

                    },
                    2800
                );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           OPEN EDIT MODAL
        ========================================================= */


        function openEditModal() {

            const data =
                getProfileData();


            if (!data) {

                return;
            }


            editName.value =
                data.name === "Not available"
                    ? ""
                    : data.name;


            editMobile.value =
                data.mobile === "Not available"
                    ? ""
                    : data.mobile;


            editCollege.value =
                data.college === "Not available"
                    ? ""
                    : data.college;


            /*
                If Department is only the default
                example, keep the input empty so
                the user can enter their real value.
            */

            editDepartment.value =
                data.department ===
                "Information Technology"
                    ? ""
                    : data.department;


            /*
                If Course is only the default
                example, keep the input empty so
                the user can enter their real value.
            */

            editCourse.value =
                data.course === "B.Tech"
                    ? ""
                    : data.course;


            editSemester.value =
                data.semester === "Not available"
                    ? ""
                    : data.semester;


            modalOverlay.classList.add(
                "show"
            );


            setTimeout(
                function () {

                    editName.focus();

                },
                100
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           CLOSE EDIT MODAL
        ========================================================= */


        function closeEditModal() {

            modalOverlay.classList.remove(
                "show"
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           SAVE PROFILE
        ========================================================= */


        function saveProfile() {

            const user =
                getLoggedInUser();


            if (!user) {

                showToast(
                    "Please login again."
                );

                return;
            }


            const newName =
                editName.value.trim();


            if (!newName) {

                showToast(
                    "Please enter your full name."
                );


                editName.focus();


                return;
            }


            /*
                Preserve original backend user object.
            */


            user.name =
                newName;


            user.fullName =
                newName;


            user.mobile =
                editMobile.value.trim();


            user.mobileNumber =
                editMobile.value.trim();


            user.college =
                editCollege.value.trim();


            user.collegeName =
                editCollege.value.trim();


            /*
                Department

                If the user leaves it empty,
                the profile will use the default
                dim example:
                Information Technology
            */

            const departmentValue =
                editDepartment.value.trim();


            if (departmentValue) {

                user.department =
                    departmentValue;

            } else {

                delete user.department;
                delete user.dept;
            }


            /*
                Course

                If the user leaves it empty,
                the profile will use the default
                dim example:
                B.Tech
            */

            const courseValue =
                editCourse.value.trim();


            if (courseValue) {

                user.course =
                    courseValue;

            } else {

                delete user.course;
                delete user.program;
                delete user.degree;
            }


            user.semester =
                editSemester.value.trim();


            /*
                Save updated profile.
            */


            sessionStorage.setItem(
                "duemate-user",
                JSON.stringify(user)
            );


            /*
                Refresh screen.
            */


            displayProfile();


            closeEditModal();


            showToast(
                "Profile updated successfully! âœ“"
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           DASHBOARD NAVIGATION
        ========================================================= */


        dashboardBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                window.location.href =
                    "dashboard.html";

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           SETTINGS NAVIGATION

           Actual file:
           settings.html
        ========================================================= */


        settingsBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                window.location.href =
                    "settings.html";

            }
        );


        settingsShortcut.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                window.location.href =
                    "settings.html";

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           EDIT PROFILE
        ========================================================= */


        editProfileBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            openEditModal
        );


        /* =========================================================
           CLOSE MODAL
        ========================================================= */


        closeModalBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            closeEditModal
        );


        cancelEditBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            closeEditModal
        );


        /* =========================================================
           CLICK OUTSIDE MODAL
        ========================================================= */


        modalOverlay.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function (event) {

                if (
                    event.target ===
                    modalOverlay
                ) {

                    closeEditModal();
                }

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           SAVE PROFILE
        ========================================================= */


        saveProfileBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            saveProfile
        );


        /* =========================================================
           ESCAPE KEY
        ========================================================= */


        document.addEventListener(
            "keydown",
            function (event) {

                if (
                    event.key === "Escape" &&
                    modalOverlay.classList.contains(
                        "show"
                    )
                ) {

                    closeEditModal();
                }

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           CHANGE PASSWORD

           Goes to existing forgot-password.html
        ========================================================= */


        changePasswordBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                window.location.href =
                    "forgot-password.html";

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           LOGOUT
        ========================================================= */


        logoutBtn.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                const confirmLogout =
                    window.confirm(
                        "Are you sure you want to logout from DueMate?"
                    );


                if (!confirmLogout) {

                    return;
                }


                sessionStorage.removeItem(
                    "duemate-user"
                );


                sessionStorage.removeItem(
                    "duemate-current-user"
                );


                window.location.replace(
                    "index.html"
                );

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           INITIALIZATION
        ========================================================= */


        applySavedTheme();

        displayProfile();

    </script>

</body>

</html>

        .security-icon {

                13px 17px;

                1px solid
                var(--border);

            border-radius: 14px;

            background:
                var(--surface-solid);

            color:
                var(--text);

            box-shadow:
                0 18px 45px
                var(--shadow);

            text-align:
                center;

            font-size:
                12px;

            opacity:
                0;

            pointer-events:
                none;

            transition:
                transform 0.35s ease,
                opacity 0.35s ease;

            z-index: 100;
        }

        .toast.show {
            transform:
                translate(-50%, 0);

            opacity: 1;
        }

                                    type="tel"

                                    name="mobile"
                                    placeholder="Enter 10-digit mobile number"
                                    autocomplete="tel"

                        ðŸ”’

    <div

        id="toast"
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
    ></div>


    <script>

        /* =========================================================
           BACKEND CONFIGURATION
        ========================================================= */

        const API_BASE_URL =
            "http://localhost:5000";


        /* =========================================================
           ELEMENTS
        ========================================================= */

        const registerForm =
            document.getElementById("registerForm");

        const nameInput =
            document.getElementById("name");

        const collegeInput =
            document.getElementById("college");

        const departmentInput =
            document.getElementById("department");

        const yearInput =
            document.getElementById("year");

        const emailInput =
            document.getElementById("email");

        const mobileInput =
            document.getElementById("mobile");

        const passwordInput =
            document.getElementById("password");

        const passwordToggle =
            document.getElementById("passwordToggle");

        const passwordStrength =
            document.getElementById("passwordStrength");

        const termsInput =
            document.getElementById("terms");

        const registerButton =
            document.getElementById("registerButton");

        const loginButton =
            document.getElementById("loginButton");

        const formMessage =
            document.getElementById("formMessage");

        const toast =
            document.getElementById("toast");

        const themeInputs =
            document.querySelectorAll(
                'input[name="theme"]'
            );


        /* =========================================================
           THEME
        ========================================================= */

        function getSelectedTheme() {

            const selected =
                document.querySelector(
                    'input[name="theme"]:checked'
                );

            return selected
                ? selected.value
                : "light";
        }


        function applyTheme(theme) {

            if (theme === "dark") {

                document.body.classList.add("dark");

                return;
            }

            if (theme === "system") {

                const prefersDark =
                    window.matchMedia(
                        "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
                    ).matches;

                document.body.classList.toggle(
                    "dark",
                    prefersDark
                );

                return;
            }

            document.body.classList.remove("dark");
        }


        /* =========================================================
           THEME PREVIEW
        ========================================================= */

        themeInputs.forEach(
            function (input) {

                input.addEventListener(
                    "change",
                    function () {

                        const theme =
                            getSelectedTheme();

                        applyTheme(theme);

                    }
                );

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           LOAD THEME
        ========================================================= */

        function loadTheme() {

            const savedTheme =
                localStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-theme"
                );

            if (
                savedTheme === "light" ||
                savedTheme === "dark" ||
                savedTheme === "system"
            ) {

                const radio =
                    document.querySelector(
                        `input[name="theme"][value="${savedTheme}"]`
                    );

                if (radio) {
                    radio.checked = true;
                }

                applyTheme(savedTheme);

            } else {

                applyTheme("light");

            }
        }

        loadTheme();


        /* =========================================================
           PASSWORD SHOW / HIDE
        ========================================================= */

        passwordToggle.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                const isPassword =
                    passwordInput.type === "password";

                if (isPassword) {

                    passwordInput.type = "text";

                    passwordToggle.textContent =
                        "ðŸ™ˆ";

                    passwordToggle.setAttribute(
                        "aria-label",
                        "Hide password"
                    );

                } else {

                    passwordInput.type =
                        "password";

                    passwordToggle.textContent =
                        "ðŸ‘ï¸";

                    passwordToggle.setAttribute(
                        "aria-label",
                        "Show password"
                    );

                }

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           PASSWORD STRENGTH
        ========================================================= */

        passwordInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            function () {

                const password =
                    passwordInput.value;

                if (!password) {

                    passwordStrength.textContent = "";
                    passwordStrength.className =
                        "password-strength";

                    return;
                }

                let score = 0;

                if (password.length >= 6) {
                    score++;
                }

                if (password.length >= 10) {
                    score++;
                }

                if (/[A-Z]/.test(password)) {
                    score++;
                }

                if (/[0-9]/.test(password)) {
                    score++;
                }

                if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(password)) {
                    score++;
                }


                if (score <= 2) {

                    passwordStrength.textContent =
                        "Password strength: Weak";

                    passwordStrength.className =
                        "password-strength weak";

                } else if (score <= 3) {

                    passwordStrength.textContent =
                        "Password strength: Medium";

                    passwordStrength.className =
                        "password-strength medium";

                } else {

                    passwordStrength.textContent =
                        "Password strength: Strong";

                    passwordStrength.className =
                        "password-strength strong";

                }

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           MESSAGE
        ========================================================= */

        function showMessage(
            text,
            type = "error"
        ) {

            formMessage.textContent =
                text;

            formMessage.className =
                "message show " + type;
        }


        function clearMessage() {

            formMessage.textContent = "";

            formMessage.className =
                "message";
        }


        /* =========================================================
           TOAST
        ========================================================= */

        let toastTimer;

        function showToast(message) {

            clearTimeout(toastTimer);

            toast.textContent =
                message;

            toast.classList.add("show");

            toastTimer =
                setTimeout(
                    function () {

                        toast.classList.remove(
                            "show"
                        );

                    },
                    2800
                );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           VALIDATION
        ========================================================= */

        function isValidEmail(email) {

            return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/
                .test(email);
        }


        function isValidMobile(mobile) {

            return /^[6-9]\d{9}$/
                .test(mobile);
        }


        /* =========================================================
           INPUT CLEANUP
        ========================================================= */

        mobileInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            function () {

                this.value =
                    this.value
                        .replace(/\D/g, "")
                        .slice(0, 10);

                clearMessage();

            }
        );


        nameInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            clearMessage
        );

        collegeInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            clearMessage
        );

        departmentInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            clearMessage
        );

        yearInput.addEventListener(
            "change",
            clearMessage
        );

        emailInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            clearMessage
        );

        passwordInput.addEventListener(
            "input",
            clearMessage
        );

        termsInput.addEventListener(
            "change",
            clearMessage
        );


        /* =========================================================
           REGISTRATION
        ========================================================= */

        registerForm.addEventListener(
            "submit",
            async function (event) {

                event.preventDefault();

                clearMessage();


                /* ================================================
                   GET VALUES
                ================================================= */

                const name =
                    nameInput.value.trim();

                const college =
                    collegeInput.value.trim();

                const department =
                    departmentInput.value.trim();

                const year =
                    yearInput.value;

                const email =
                    emailInput.value
                        .trim()
                        .toLowerCase();

                const mobile =
                    mobileInput.value.trim();

                const password =
                    passwordInput.value;

                const theme =
                    getSelectedTheme();


                /* ================================================
                   VALIDATION
                ================================================= */

                if (!name) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please enter your full name."
                    );

                    nameInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (name.length < 2) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please enter a valid name."
                    );

                    nameInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!college) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please enter your college name."
                    );

                    collegeInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!department) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please enter your department."
                    );

                    departmentInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!year) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please select your year of study."
                    );

                    yearInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!email) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please enter your email ID."
                    );

                    emailInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!isValidEmail(email)) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please enter a valid email ID."
                    );

                    emailInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!mobile) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please enter your mobile number."
                    );

                    mobileInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!isValidMobile(mobile)) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please enter a valid 10-digit mobile number."
                    );

                    mobileInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!password) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please create a password."
                    );

                    passwordInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (password.length < 6) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Password must contain at least 6 characters."
                    );

                    passwordInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                if (!termsInput.checked) {

                    showMessage(
                        "Please agree to the Terms & Conditions."
                    );

                    termsInput.focus();

                    return;
                }


                /* ================================================
                   LOADING
                ================================================= */

                registerButton.classList.add(
                    "loading"
                );

                registerButton.textContent =
                    "Creating account...";


                /* ================================================
                   BACKEND REQUEST
                ================================================= */

                try {

                    const response =
                        await fetch(
                            API_BASE_URL +
                            "/api/auth/register",
                            {
                                method: "POST",

                                headers: {
                                    "Content-Type":
                                        "application/json"
                                },

                                body:
                                    JSON.stringify({

                                        name:
                                            name,

                                        college:
                                            college,

                                        department:
                                            department,

                                        year:
                                            year,

                                        email:
                                            email,

                                        mobile:
                                            mobile,

                                        password:
                                            password,

                                        theme:
                                            theme

                                    })
                            }
                        );


                    /* ============================================
                       READ SERVER RESPONSE
                    ============================================= */

                    let data = {};

                    const responseText =
                        await response.text();

                    if (responseText) {

                        try {

                            data =
                                JSON.parse(
                                    responseText
                                );

                        } catch (error) {

                            data = {
                                message:
                                    responseText
                            };

                        }

                    }


                    /* ============================================
                       ERROR RESPONSE
                    ============================================= */

                    if (!response.ok) {

                        throw new Error(
                            data.message ||
                            "Registration failed. Please try again."
                        );

                    }


                    /* ============================================
                       SAVE THEME
                    ============================================= */

                    localStorage.setItem(
                        "duemate-theme",
                        theme
                    );


                    /* ============================================
                       SAVE BASIC USER INFORMATION
                       ONLY FOR FRONTEND USE
                    ============================================= */

                    localStorage.setItem(
                        "duemate-user-email",
                        email
                    );


                    /* ============================================
                       SUCCESS
                    ============================================= */

                    showMessage(
                        "Account created successfully! Redirecting to login...",
                        "success"
                    );

                    showToast(
                        "Welcome to DueMate! ðŸŽ‰"
                    );


                    /* ============================================
                       REDIRECT TO LOGIN
                    ============================================= */

                    setTimeout(
                        function () {

                            window.location.href =
                                "index.html";

                        },
                        1200
                    );

                }


                /* ================================================
                   ERROR
                ================================================= */

                catch (error) {

                    console.error(
                        "Registration error:",
                        error
                    );


                    let errorMessage =
                        error.message ||
                        "Unable to create your account.";


                    if (
                        error instanceof TypeError
                    ) {

                        errorMessage =
                            "Unable to connect to the DueMate server. Please make sure the backend is running on port 5000.";

                    }


                    showMessage(
                        errorMessage
                    );

                }


                /* ================================================
                   RESET BUTTON
                ================================================= */

                finally {

                    registerButton.classList.remove(
                        "loading"
                    );

                    registerButton.textContent =
                        "Create Account";

                }

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           LOGIN NAVIGATION
        ========================================================= */

        loginButton.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                window.location.href =
                    "index.html";

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           TERMS
        ========================================================= */

        document
            .getElementById("termsLink")
            .addEventListener(
                "click",
                function (event) {

                    event.preventDefault();

                    showToast(
                        "DueMate Terms & Conditions will be available here."
                    );

                }
            );


        /* =========================================================
           SYSTEM THEME CHANGE
        ========================================================= */

        const systemTheme =
            window.matchMedia(
                "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
            );


        systemTheme.addEventListener(
            "change",
            function () {

                if (
                    getSelectedTheme() === "system"
                ) {

                    applyTheme("system");

                }

            }
        );


        /* =========================================================
           INITIAL FOCUS
        ========================================================= */

        window.addEventListener(
            "load",
            function () {

                if (
                    window.innerWidth > 650
                ) {

                    nameInput.focus();

                }

            }
        );

    </script>

</body>

</html>

         TOAST

    <div
        class="toast"
        id="toast"
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
    ></div>


    <!-- =========================================================
         JAVASCRIPT
    ========================================================= -->

    <script>

        /* =========================================================
           AUTHENTICATION CHECK

           IMPORTANT:
           Login page stores:

           sessionStorage.setItem(
               "duemate-user",
               JSON.stringify(data.user)
           );

           Therefore Settings uses THE SAME KEY.
        ========================================================= */

        const loggedInUser =
            sessionStorage.getItem("duemate-user");

        if (!loggedInUser) {

            alert("Please login to access Settings.");

            window.location.href = "index.html";
        }


        /* =========================================================
           ELEMENTS
        ========================================================= */

        const toast =
            document.getElementById("toast");


        /* =========================================================
           USER-SPECIFIC SETTINGS KEY

           Each account gets separate settings.
        ========================================================= */

        let currentUser = {};

        try {

            currentUser =
                JSON.parse(loggedInUser || "{}");

        } catch (error) {

            currentUser = {};
        }

        const userEmail =
            currentUser.email ||
            "default-user";

        const settingsKey =
            "duemate-settings-" +
            userEmail;


        /* =========================================================
           DEFAULT SETTINGS
        ========================================================= */

        const defaultSettings = {

            language: "English",

            startPage: "dashboard.html",

            compact: false,

            theme: "light",

            taskReminders: true,

            deadlineAlerts: true,

            groupNotifications: true,

            dailySummary: false,

            profileVisibility: "private",

            activityVisibility: false
        };


        /* =========================================================
           LOAD SETTINGS
        ========================================================= */

        function loadSettings() {

            let saved = {};

            try {

                saved =
                    JSON.parse(
                        localStorage.getItem(
                            settingsKey
                        )
                    ) || {};

            } catch (error) {

                saved = {};
            }

            return {
                ...defaultSettings,
                ...saved
            };
        }


        let settings =
            loadSettings();


        /* =========================================================
           SAVE SETTINGS INTERNALLY
        ========================================================= */

        function saveSettingsData() {

            localStorage.setItem(
                settingsKey,
                JSON.stringify(settings)
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           APPLY THEME
        ========================================================= */

        function applyTheme(theme) {

            if (theme === "dark") {

                document.body.classList.add("dark");

            } else if (theme === "light") {

                document.body.classList.remove("dark");

            } else {

                const systemDark =
                    window.matchMedia(
                        "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
                    ).matches;

                document.body.classList.toggle(
                    "dark",
                    systemDark
                );
            }

            localStorage.setItem(
                "duemate-theme",
                theme
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           SELECT THEME
        ========================================================= */

        function selectTheme(theme) {

            settings.theme = theme;

            applyTheme(theme);

            updateThemeSelection();

            saveSettingsData();

            showToast(
                "Appearance updated successfully."
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           UPDATE THEME UI
        ========================================================= */

        function updateThemeSelection() {

            document
                .getElementById("lightTheme")
                .classList.remove("selected");

            document
                .getElementById("darkTheme")
                .classList.remove("selected");

            document
                .getElementById("systemTheme")
                .classList.remove("selected");


            if (settings.theme === "dark") {

                document
                    .getElementById("darkTheme")
                    .classList.add("selected");

            } else if (settings.theme === "system") {

                document
                    .getElementById("systemTheme")
                    .classList.add("selected");

            } else {

                document
                    .getElementById("lightTheme")
                    .classList.add("selected");
            }
        }


        /* =========================================================
           LOAD FORM VALUES
        ========================================================= */

        function populateSettings() {

            document.getElementById(
                "languageSelect"
            ).value =
                settings.language;

            document.getElementById(
                "startPageSelect"
            ).value =
                settings.startPage;

            document.getElementById(
                "compactToggle"
            ).checked =
                settings.compact;

            document.getElementById(
                "taskReminderToggle"
            ).checked =
                settings.taskReminders;

            document.getElementById(
                "deadlineToggle"
            ).checked =
                settings.deadlineAlerts;

            document.getElementById(
                "groupNotificationToggle"
            ).checked =
                settings.groupNotifications;

            document.getElementById(
                "dailySummaryToggle"
            ).checked =
                settings.dailySummary;

            document.getElementById(
                "profileVisibility"
            ).value =
                settings.profileVisibility;

            document.getElementById(
                "activityToggle"
            ).checked =
                settings.activityVisibility;

            updateThemeSelection();
        }


        /* =========================================================
           SAVE SETTINGS BUTTON
        ========================================================= */

        function saveSettings() {

            settings.language =
                document.getElementById(
                    "languageSelect"
                ).value;

            settings.startPage =
                document.getElementById(
                    "startPageSelect"
                ).value;

            settings.compact =
                document.getElementById(
                    "compactToggle"
                ).checked;

            settings.taskReminders =
                document.getElementById(
                    "taskReminderToggle"
                ).checked;

            settings.deadlineAlerts =
                document.getElementById(
                    "deadlineToggle"
                ).checked;

            settings.groupNotifications =
                document.getElementById(
                    "groupNotificationToggle"
                ).checked;

            settings.dailySummary =
                document.getElementById(
                    "dailySummaryToggle"
                ).checked;

            settings.profileVisibility =
                document.getElementById(
                    "profileVisibility"
                ).value;

            settings.activityVisibility =
                document.getElementById(
                    "activityToggle"
                ).checked;


            saveSettingsData();

            applyTheme(settings.theme);

            showToast(
                "Settings saved successfully! âœ…"
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           SECTION SWITCHING
        ========================================================= */

        function showSection(
            sectionId,
            clickedButton
        ) {

            const sections =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".settings-section"
                );

            sections.forEach(
                function(section) {

                    section.style.display =
                        "none";
                }
            );


            const selected =
                document.getElementById(
                    sectionId
                );

            if (selected) {

                selected.style.display =
                    "block";
            }


            const buttons =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".menu-btn"
                );

            buttons.forEach(
                function(button) {

                    button.classList.remove(
                        "active"
                    );
                }
            );


            clickedButton.classList.add(
                "active"
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           DASHBOARD
        ========================================================= */

        function goDashboard() {

            window.location.href =
                "dashboard.html";
        }


        /* =========================================================
           PROFILE

           IMPORTANT:
           Profile is a separate page.
           Settings does NOT contain profile details.
        ========================================================= */

        function goProfile() {

            window.location.href =
                "profile.html";
        }


        /* =========================================================
           LOGOUT
        ========================================================= */

        function logout() {

            const confirmed =
                confirm(
                    "Are you sure you want to logout?"
                );

            if (!confirmed) {
                return;
            }

            sessionStorage.removeItem(
                "duemate-user"
            );

            window.location.href =
                "index.html";
        }


        /* =========================================================
           CLEAR PREFERENCES
        ========================================================= */

        function clearPreferences() {

            const confirmed =
                confirm(
                    "Clear your saved DueMate settings?"
                );

            if (!confirmed) {
                return;
            }

            localStorage.removeItem(
                settingsKey
            );

            localStorage.removeItem(
                "duemate-theme"
            );

            settings =
                {
                    ...defaultSettings
                };

            populateSettings();

            applyTheme(
                settings.theme
            );

            showToast(
                "Preferences cleared."
            );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           TOAST
        ========================================================= */

        let toastTimer;

        function showToast(message) {

            clearTimeout(toastTimer);

            toast.textContent =
                message;

            toast.classList.add(
                "show"
            );

            toastTimer =
                setTimeout(
                    function() {

                        toast.classList.remove(
                            "show"
                        );

                    },
                    2500
                );
        }


        /* =========================================================
           SYSTEM THEME CHANGE
        ========================================================= */

        window
            .matchMedia(
                "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
            )
            .addEventListener(
                "change",
                function() {

                    if (
                        settings.theme ===
                        "system"
                    ) {

                        applyTheme(
                            "system"
                        );
                    }
                }
            );


        /* =========================================================
           INITIALIZE
        ========================================================= */

        applyTheme(
            settings.theme
        );

        populateSettings();

    </script>

</body>
</html>
