    <script>

        /* =====================================================
           THEME
           ===================================================== */

        function applySavedTheme() {

            const savedTheme =
                localStorage.getItem("duemate-theme") || "light";


            if (savedTheme === "dark") {

                document.body.classList.add("dark");

            } else {

                document.body.classList.remove("dark");
            }
        }


        applySavedTheme();



        /* =====================================================
           DATE
           ===================================================== */

        function formatShortDate(date) {

            return date.toLocaleDateString(
                undefined,
                {
                    day: "numeric",
                    month: "short",
                    year: "numeric"
                }
            );
        }


        function updateDate() {

            const currentDate =
                document.getElementById(
                    "currentDate"
                );


            if (currentDate) {

                currentDate.textContent =
                    formatShortDate(
                        new Date()
                    );
            }
        }


        updateDate();



        /* =====================================================
           STORAGE KEY
           ===================================================== */

        function getUserEmail() {

            return (
                sessionStorage.getItem(
                    "duemate-user-email"
                ) || "guest"
            );
        }


        function getSubjectStorageKey() {

            return (
                "duemate-subjects-" +
                getUserEmail().toLowerCase()
            );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           SUBJECT DATA
           ===================================================== */

        let subjects = [];

        let editingSubjectId = null;

        let selectedColor = "#756cff";



        /* =====================================================
           LOAD SUBJECTS
           ===================================================== */

        function loadSubjects() {

            try {

                const stored =
                    sessionStorage.getItem(
                        getSubjectStorageKey()
                    );


                if (!stored) {

                    subjects = [];

                    return;
                }


                const parsed =
                    JSON.parse(stored);


                if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {

                    subjects = parsed;

                } else {

                    subjects = [];
                }

            } catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "Unable to load subjects:",
                    error
                );

                subjects = [];
            }
        }



        /* =====================================================
           SAVE SUBJECTS
           ===================================================== */

        function saveSubjectsToStorage() {

            sessionStorage.setItem(
                getSubjectStorageKey(),
                JSON.stringify(subjects)
            );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           ESCAPE HTML
           ===================================================== */

        function escapeHTML(value) {

            const div =
                document.createElement("div");


            div.textContent =
                String(value ?? "");


            return div.innerHTML;
        }



        /* =====================================================
           RENDER SUBJECTS
           ===================================================== */

        function renderSubjects(list = subjects) {

            const grid =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectsGrid"
                );


            if (!grid) {

                return;
            }


            if (!list.length) {

                const hasSearch =
                    document.getElementById(
                        "searchInput"
                    )?.value.trim();


                if (hasSearch) {

                    grid.innerHTML = `

                        <div class="empty-state">

                            <div class="empty-icon">
                                ðŸ”Ž
                            </div>

                            <h3>
                                No subjects found
                            </h3>

                            <p>
                                Try a different subject name,
                                code or faculty name.
                            </p>

                        </div>

                    `;

                } else {

                    grid.innerHTML = `

                        <div class="empty-state">

                            <div class="empty-icon">
                                ðŸ“š
                            </div>

                            <h3>
                                No subjects added yet
                            </h3>

                            <p>
                                Add your first subject to start
                                organizing your academic work.
                            </p>

                            <button
                                class="empty-add-btn"
                                type="button"
                                onclick="openAddModal()">

                                ï¼‹ Add Your First Subject

                            </button>

                        </div>

                    `;
                }

                return;
            }


            grid.innerHTML =
                list.map(
                    function(subject) {

                        return `

                            <article
                                class="subject-card"
                                style="--card-color:${escapeHTML(subject.color || "#756cff")}">


                                <div class="subject-card-top">


                                    <div class="subject-icon">

                                        ðŸ“š

                                    </div>


                                    <div class="subject-actions">


                                        <button
                                            class="subject-action"
                                            type="button"
                                            title="Edit subject"
                                            aria-label="Edit subject"
                                            onclick="editSubject('${escapeHTML(subject.id)}')">

                                            âœï¸

                                        </button>


                                        <button
                                            class="subject-action delete"
                                            type="button"
                                            title="Delete subject"
                                            aria-label="Delete subject"
                                            onclick="deleteSubject('${escapeHTML(subject.id)}')">

                                            ðŸ—‘ï¸

                                        </button>

                                    </div>

                                </div>



                                <div class="subject-name">

                                    ${escapeHTML(subject.name)}

                                </div>


                                ${
                                    subject.code
                                    ?
                                    `
                                    <div class="subject-code">

                                        ${escapeHTML(subject.code)}

                                    </div>
                                    `
                                    :
                                    ""
                                }



                                <div class="subject-info">


                                    ${
                                        subject.faculty
                                        ?
                                        `
                                        <div class="info-row">

                                            <span class="info-row-icon">
                                                ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ«
                                            </span>

                                            <span>
                                                ${escapeHTML(subject.faculty)}
                                            </span>

                                        </div>
                                        `
                                        :
                                        ""
                                    }


                                    <div class="info-row">

                                        <span class="info-row-icon">
                                            ðŸ“Œ
                                        </span>

                                        <span>
                                            Academic Subject
                                        </span>

                                    </div>

                                </div>



                                ${
                                    subject.notes
                                    ?
                                    `
                                    <div class="subject-notes">

                                        ðŸ“ ${escapeHTML(subject.notes)}

                                    </div>
                                    `
                                    :
                                    ""
                                }

                            </article>

                        `;
                    }
                ).join("");
        }



        /* =====================================================
           OPEN ADD MODAL
           ===================================================== */

        function openAddModal() {

            editingSubjectId = null;

            const modalTitle =
                document.getElementById(
                    "modalTitle"
                );


            if (modalTitle) {

                modalTitle.textContent =
                    "Add Subject";
            }


            resetSubjectForm();


            const modal =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectModal"
                );


            if (modal) {

                modal.classList.add("show");
            }


            setTimeout(
                function() {

                    const input =
                        document.getElementById(
                            "subjectName"
                        );


                    if (input) {

                        input.focus();
                    }

                },
                100
            );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           EDIT SUBJECT
           ===================================================== */

        function editSubject(id) {

            const subject =
                subjects.find(
                    function(item) {

                        return item.id === id;
                    }
                );


            if (!subject) {

                return;
            }


            editingSubjectId =
                id;


            document.getElementById(
                "modalTitle"
            ).textContent =
                "Edit Subject";


            document.getElementById(
                "subjectName"
            ).value =
                subject.name || "";


            document.getElementById(
                "subjectCode"
            ).value =
                subject.code || "";


            document.getElementById(
                "facultyName"
            ).value =
                subject.faculty || "";


            document.getElementById(
                "subjectNotes"
            ).value =
                subject.notes || "";


            selectedColor =
                subject.color || "#756cff";


            updateColorSelection();


            document.getElementById(
                "subjectModal"
            ).classList.add("show");


            setTimeout(
                function() {

                    document.getElementById(
                        "subjectName"
                    ).focus();

                },
                100
            );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           CLOSE MODAL
           ===================================================== */

        function closeSubjectModal() {

            const modal =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectModal"
                );


            if (modal) {

                modal.classList.remove("show");
            }


            editingSubjectId = null;

            resetSubjectForm();
        }



        /* =====================================================
           RESET FORM
           ===================================================== */

        function resetSubjectForm() {

            const form =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectForm"
                );


            if (form) {

                form.reset();
            }


            selectedColor =
                "#756cff";


            updateColorSelection();
        }



        /* =====================================================
           COLOR SELECTION
           ===================================================== */

        function selectColor(element) {

            if (!element) {

                return;
            }


            selectedColor =
                element.dataset.color ||
                "#756cff";


            updateColorSelection();
        }


        function updateColorSelection() {

            const options =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".color-option"
                );


            options.forEach(
                function(option) {

                    if (
                        option.dataset.color ===
                        selectedColor
                    ) {

                        option.classList.add(
                            "selected"
                        );

                    } else {

                        option.classList.remove(
                            "selected"
                        );
                    }
                }
            );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           SAVE SUBJECT
           ===================================================== */

        function saveSubject(event) {

            event.preventDefault();


            const name =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectName"
                ).value.trim();


            const code =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectCode"
                ).value.trim();


            const faculty =
                document.getElementById(
                    "facultyName"
                ).value.trim();


            const notes =
                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectNotes"
                ).value.trim();


            if (!name) {

                showToast(
                    "Please enter the subject name."
                );

                document.getElementById(
                    "subjectName"
                ).focus();

                return;
            }


            const duplicate =
                subjects.some(
                    function(subject) {

                        const sameName =
                            subject.name
                                .toLowerCase() ===
                            name.toLowerCase();


                        return (
                            sameName &&
                            subject.id !==
                                editingSubjectId
                        );
                    }
                );


            if (duplicate) {

                showToast(
                    "This subject already exists."
                );

                return;
            }


            if (editingSubjectId) {

                const index =
                    subjects.findIndex(
                        function(subject) {

                            return (
                                subject.id ===
                                editingSubjectId
                            );
                        }
                    );


                if (index !== -1) {

                    subjects[index] = {

                        ...subjects[index],

                        name:
                            name,

                        code:
                            code,

                        faculty:
                            faculty,

                        notes:
                            notes,

                        color:
                            selectedColor,

                        updatedAt:
                            new Date().toISOString()
                    };
                }


                showToast(
                    "Subject updated successfully."
                );

            } else {

                const newSubject = {

                    id:
                        createSubjectId(),

                    name:
                        name,

                    code:
                        code,

                    faculty:
                        faculty,

                    notes:
                        notes,

                    color:
                        selectedColor,

                    createdAt:
                        new Date().toISOString()
                };


                subjects.unshift(
                    newSubject
                );


                showToast(
                    "Subject added successfully."
                );
            }


            saveSubjectsToStorage();

            renderSubjects();

            closeSubjectModal();
        }



        /* =====================================================
           CREATE ID
           ===================================================== */

        function createSubjectId() {

            return (
                "subject-" +
                Date.now() +
                "-" +
                Math.random()
                    .toString(36)
                    .substring(2, 9)
            );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           DELETE SUBJECT
           ===================================================== */

        function deleteSubject(id) {

            const subject =
                subjects.find(
                    function(item) {

                        return item.id === id;
                    }
                );


            if (!subject) {

                return;
            }


            const confirmed =
                confirm(
                    `Delete "${subject.name}"?`
                );


            if (!confirmed) {

                return;
            }


            subjects =
                subjects.filter(
                    function(item) {

                        return item.id !== id;
                    }
                );


            saveSubjectsToStorage();

            applySearch();

            showToast(
                "Subject deleted."
            );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           SEARCH
           ===================================================== */

        function applySearch() {

            const input =
                document.getElementById(
                    "searchInput"
                );


            const query =
                input
                    ?
                    input.value
                        .trim()
                        .toLowerCase()
                    :
                    "";


            if (!query) {

                renderSubjects(
                    subjects
                );

                return;
            }


            const filtered =
                subjects.filter(
                    function(subject) {

                        return (

                            String(
                                subject.name || ""
                            )
                                .toLowerCase()
                                .includes(query)

                            ||

                            String(
                                subject.code || ""
                            )
                                .toLowerCase()
                                .includes(query)

                            ||

                            String(
                                subject.faculty || ""
                            )
                                .toLowerCase()
                                .includes(query)

                        );
                    }
                );


            renderSubjects(
                filtered
            );
        }



        document.getElementById(
            "searchInput"
        ).addEventListener(
            "input",
            applySearch
        );



        /* =====================================================
           DRAWER
           ===================================================== */

        function openDrawer() {

            const drawer =
                document.getElementById(
                    "drawer"
                );


            const overlay =
                document.getElementById(
                    "drawerOverlay"
                );


            drawer.classList.add(
                "show"
            );


            overlay.classList.add(
                "show"
            );
        }


        function closeDrawer() {

            const drawer =
                document.getElementById(
                    "drawer"
                );


            const overlay =
                document.getElementById(
                    "drawerOverlay"
                );


            drawer.classList.remove(
                "show"
            );


            overlay.classList.remove(
                "show"
            );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           NAVIGATION
           ===================================================== */

        function openCalendar() {

            window.location.href =
                "calendar.html";
        }


        function openNotifications() {

            window.location.href =
                "notifications.html";
        }



        /* =====================================================
           LOGOUT
           ===================================================== */

        function logout() {

            const confirmed =
                confirm(
                    "Are you sure you want to logout?"
                );


            if (!confirmed) {

                return;
            }


            sessionStorage.removeItem(
                "duemate-user"
            );


            sessionStorage.removeItem(
                "duemate-user-name"
            );


            sessionStorage.removeItem(
                "duemate-user-email"
            );


            window.location.href =
                "index.html";
        }



        /* =====================================================
           TOAST
           ===================================================== */

        let toastTimer;


        function showToast(message) {

            const toast =
                document.getElementById(
                    "toast"
                );


            if (!toast) {

                return;
            }


            toast.textContent =
                message;


            toast.classList.add(
                "show"
            );


            clearTimeout(
                toastTimer
            );


            toastTimer =
                setTimeout(
                    function() {

                        toast.classList.remove(
                            "show"
                        );

                    },
                    2500
                );
        }



        /* =====================================================
           ESC KEY
           ===================================================== */

        document.addEventListener(
            "keydown",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    event.key ===
                    "Escape"
                ) {

                    closeDrawer();

                    closeSubjectModal();
                }

            }
        );



        /* =====================================================
           CLICK OUTSIDE MODAL
           ===================================================== */

        document.getElementById(
            "subjectModal"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    event.target ===
                    this
                ) {

                    closeSubjectModal();
                }

            }
        );



        /* =====================================================
           BACKGROUND PARALLAX
           ===================================================== */

        let mouseX = 0;

        let mouseY = 0;

        let smoothX = 0;

        let smoothY = 0;


        document.addEventListener(
            "mousemove",
            function(event) {

                if (
                    window.innerWidth <=
                    700
                ) {

                    return;
                }


                mouseX =
                    event.clientX /
                    window.innerWidth -
                    0.5;


                mouseY =
                    event.clientY /
                    window.innerHeight -
                    0.5;

            }
        );


        function animateBackground() {

            smoothX +=
                (
                    mouseX -
                    smoothX
                ) * 0.025;


            smoothY +=
                (
                    mouseY -
                    smoothY
                ) * 0.025;


            const glows =
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".glow"
                );


            glows.forEach(
                function(glow, index) {

                    const depth =
                        (index + 1) * 5;


                    glow.style.marginLeft =
                        `${smoothX * depth}px`;


                    glow.style.marginTop =
                        `${smoothY * depth}px`;

                }
            );


            requestAnimationFrame(
                animateBackground
            );
        }


        animateBackground();



        /* =====================================================
           INITIALIZE
           ===================================================== */

        loadSubjects();

        renderSubjects();

        updateColorSelection();

    </script>

</body>

</html>

    <script>

        /* =====================================================
           DUE MATE TASKS
        ===================================================== */

        const API_BASE = "http://localhost:5000";

        let tasks = [];
        let currentFilter = "all";
        let editingTaskId = null;


        /* =====================================================
           USER
        ===================================================== */

        function getCurrentUser() {

            const possibleKeys = [
                "dueMateUser",
                "currentUser",
                "loggedInUser",
                "user",
                "userEmail",
                "email"
            ];

            for (const key of possibleKeys) {

                const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(key);

                if (!raw) {
                    continue;
                }

                try {

                    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);

                    if (
                        parsed &&
                        typeof parsed === "object"
                    ) {
                        return parsed;
                    }

                } catch (error) {

                    if (
                        String(raw).includes("@") &&
                        String(raw).includes(".")
                    ) {

                        return {
                            email: String(raw)
                        };

                    }

                }

            }

            return null;

        }


        function getUserEmail() {

            const user = getCurrentUser();

            if (!user) {
                return "";
            }

            return String(
                user.email ||
                user.user_email ||
                user.emailId ||
                user.userEmail ||
                ""
            )
                .trim()
                .toLowerCase();

        }


        const currentUser = getCurrentUser();
        const userEmail = getUserEmail();


        /* =====================================================
           AUTH CHECK
        ===================================================== */

        if (!currentUser || !userEmail) {

            document.body.innerHTML = `
                <div style="
                    min-height:100vh;
                    display:flex;
                    align-items:center;
                    justify-content:center;
                    font-family:Arial;
                    padding:20px;
                    text-align:center;
                ">

                    <div>

                        <div style="
                            font-size:50px;
                            margin-bottom:15px;
                        ">
                            ðŸ”
                        </div>

                        <h2>
                            Login required
                        </h2>

                        <p style="
                            margin-top:8px;
                            color:#687386;
                        ">
                            Please login to access your tasks.
                        </p>

                        <button
                            onclick="window.location.href='index.html'"
                            style="
                                margin-top:20px;
                                padding:12px 20px;
                                border:0;
                                border-radius:10px;
                                background:#6c63ff;
                                color:white;
                                font-weight:bold;
                                cursor:pointer;
                            "
                        >
                            Go to Login
                        </button>

                    </div>

                </div>
            `;

        }


        /* =====================================================
           THEME
        ===================================================== */

        function applyTheme(theme) {

            if (!theme) {
                theme = "system";
            }

            document.body.classList.remove("dark");

            if (theme === "dark") {

                document.body.classList.add("dark");

            }

            else if (theme === "system") {

                if (
                    window.matchMedia &&
                    window.matchMedia(
                        "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
                    ).matches
                ) {

                    document.body.classList.add("dark");

                }

            }

        }


        function loadTheme() {

            let theme = "system";

            const user = getCurrentUser();

            if (user && user.theme) {
                theme = user.theme;
            }

            const storedTheme =
                sessionStorage.getItem("dueMateTheme");

            if (storedTheme) {
                theme = storedTheme;
            }

            applyTheme(theme);

        }


        loadTheme();


        /* =====================================================
           LOAD TASKS
        ===================================================== */

        async function loadTasks() {

            if (!userEmail) {
                return;
            }

            const taskList =
                document.getElementById("taskList");

            taskList.innerHTML = `
                <div class="empty">
                    <div class="empty-icon">â³</div>
                    <p>Loading your tasks...</p>
                </div>
            `;

            try {

                const response = await fetch(
                    `${API_BASE}/api/tasks?user_email=${encodeURIComponent(userEmail)}`
                );

                const data = await response.json();

                if (!response.ok) {

                    throw new Error(
                        data.message ||
                        "Unable to load tasks."
                    );

                }

                if (Array.isArray(data)) {

                    tasks = data;

                }

                else if (Array.isArray(data.tasks)) {

                    tasks = data.tasks;

                }

                else {

                    tasks = [];

                }

                renderTasks();
                updateStats();

            }

            catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "Load tasks error:",
                    error
                );

                taskList.innerHTML = `

                    <div class="empty">

                        <div class="empty-icon">
                            âš ï¸
                        </div>

                        <p>
                            Unable to load tasks.
                        </p>

                        <small>
                            Make sure the DueMate backend is running on port 5000.
                        </small>

                    </div>

                `;

            }

        }


        /* =====================================================
           DATE FORMAT
        ===================================================== */

        function formatDate(value) {

            if (!value) {
                return "";
            }

            const date =
                new Date(`${String(value).substring(0, 10)}T00:00:00`);

            if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
                return String(value);
            }

            return date.toLocaleDateString(
                "en-IN",
                {
                    day: "numeric",
                    month: "short",
                    year: "numeric"
                }
            );

        }


        /* =====================================================
           FILTER
        ===================================================== */

        function getFilteredTasks() {

            if (currentFilter === "pending") {

                return tasks.filter(
                    task => Number(task.completed) !== 1
                );

            }

            if (currentFilter === "completed") {

                return tasks.filter(
                    task => Number(task.completed) === 1
                );

            }

            return tasks;

        }


        /* =====================================================
           RENDER TASKS
        ===================================================== */

        function renderTasks() {

            const taskList =
                document.getElementById("taskList");

            const filteredTasks =
                getFilteredTasks();

            document.getElementById(
                "taskCount"
            ).textContent =
                `${filteredTasks.length} ${
                    filteredTasks.length === 1
                        ? "task"
                        : "tasks"
                }`;


            if (filteredTasks.length === 0) {

                taskList.innerHTML = `

                    <div class="empty">

                        <div class="empty-icon">
                            ðŸ“š
                        </div>

                        <p>
                            No tasks found.
                        </p>

                        <small>
                            Add a task to get started.
                        </small>

                    </div>

                `;

                return;

            }


            taskList.innerHTML =
                filteredTasks.map(task => {

                    const completed =
                        Number(task.completed) === 1;

                    const priority =
                        ["low", "medium", "high"].includes(
                            String(task.priority || "").toLowerCase()
                        )
                            ? String(task.priority).toLowerCase()
                            : "medium";


                    return `

                        <div
                            class="task-item"
                            data-id="${escapeHtml(task.id)}"
                        >

                            <button
                                class="task-check ${
                                    completed
                                        ? "completed"
                                        : ""
                                }"
                                onclick="toggleTask('${escapeJs(task.id)}')"
                                title="${
                                    completed
                                        ? "Mark as pending"
                                        : "Mark as completed"
                                }"
                                type="button"
                            >
                                ${
                                    completed
                                        ? "âœ“"
                                        : ""
                                }
                            </button>


                            <div class="task-main">

                                <div
                                    class="task-name ${
                                        completed
                                            ? "completed"
                                            : ""
                                    }"
                                >
                                    ${escapeHtml(task.name)}
                                </div>


                                <div class="task-meta">

                                    ${
                                        task.subject
                                            ? `
                                                <span>
                                                    ðŸ“š
                                                    ${escapeHtml(task.subject)}
                                                </span>
                                              `
                                            : ""
                                    }

                                    ${
                                        task.task_date || task.date
                                            ? `
                                                <span>
                                                    ðŸ“…
                                                    ${formatDate(
                                                        task.task_date ||
                                                        task.date
                                                    )}
                                                </span>
                                              `
                                            : ""
                                    }

                                    <span class="priority ${priority}">
                                        ${capitalize(priority)}
                                    </span>

                                </div>

                            </div>


                            <div class="task-actions">

                                <button
                                    class="small-btn"
                                    onclick="editTask('${escapeJs(task.id)}')"
                                    title="Edit task"
                                    type="button"
                                >
                                    âœï¸
                                </button>

                                <button
                                    class="small-btn delete"
                                    onclick="deleteTask('${escapeJs(task.id)}')"
                                    title="Delete task"
                                    type="button"
                                >
                                    ðŸ—‘ï¸
                                </button>

                            </div>

                        </div>

                    `;

                }).join("");

        }


        /* =====================================================
           STATS
        ===================================================== */

        function updateStats() {

            const total =
                tasks.length;

            const completed =
                tasks.filter(
                    task => Number(task.completed) === 1
                ).length;

            const pending =
                total - completed;

            const high =
                tasks.filter(
                    task =>
                        String(
                            task.priority || ""
                        ).toLowerCase() === "high"
                ).length;


            document.getElementById(
                "totalTasks"
            ).textContent = total;

            document.getElementById(
                "pendingTasks"
            ).textContent = pending;

            document.getElementById(
                "completedTasks"
            ).textContent = completed;

            document.getElementById(
                "highTasks"
            ).textContent = high;

        }


        /* =====================================================
           OPEN ADD MODAL
        ===================================================== */

        function openAddModal() {

            editingTaskId = null;

            document.getElementById(
                "modalTitle"
            ).textContent =
                "Add Task";

            document.getElementById(
                "saveBtn"
            ).textContent =
                "Save Task";

            document.getElementById(
                "taskForm"
            ).reset();

            document.getElementById(
                "taskId"
            ).value = "";

            document.getElementById(
                "taskPriority"
            ).value =
                "medium";


            const today =
                new Date();

            const yyyy =
                today.getFullYear();

            const mm =
                String(
                    today.getMonth() + 1
                ).padStart(2, "0");

            const dd =
                String(
                    today.getDate()
                ).padStart(2, "0");


            document.getElementById(
                "taskDate"
            ).value =
                `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;


            document.getElementById(
                "taskModal"
            ).classList.add("show");

        }


        /* =====================================================
           EDIT TASK
        ===================================================== */

        function editTask(id) {

            const task =
                tasks.find(
                    item =>
                        String(item.id) ===
                        String(id)
                );

            if (!task) {
                return;
            }


            editingTaskId =
                task.id;


            document.getElementById(
                "modalTitle"
            ).textContent =
                "Edit Task";


            document.getElementById(
                "saveBtn"
            ).textContent =
                "Update Task";


            document.getElementById(
                "taskId"
            ).value =
                task.id;


            document.getElementById(
                "taskName"
            ).value =
                task.name || "";


            document.getElementById(
                "taskSubject"
            ).value =
                task.subject || "";


            document.getElementById(
                "taskDate"
            ).value =
                String(
                    task.task_date ||
                    task.date ||
                    ""
                ).substring(0, 10);


            document.getElementById(
                "taskPriority"
            ).value =
                task.priority || "medium";


            document.getElementById(
                "taskDescription"
            ).value =
                task.description || "";


            document.getElementById(
                "taskModal"
            ).classList.add("show");

        }


        /* =====================================================
           SAVE TASK
        ===================================================== */

        async function saveTask(event) {

            event.preventDefault();


            const name =
                document.getElementById(
                    "taskName"
                ).value.trim();


            const subject =
                document.getElementById(
                    "taskSubject"
                ).value.trim();


            const date =
                document.getElementById(
                    "taskDate"
                ).value;


            const priority =
                document.getElementById(
                    "taskPriority"
                ).value;


            const description =
                document.getElementById(
                    "taskDescription"
                ).value.trim();


            if (!name) {

                alert(
                    "Please enter a task name."
                );

                return;

            }


            if (!date) {

                alert(
                    "Please select a task date."
                );

                return;

            }


            const payload = {

                user_email:
                    userEmail,

                name:
                    name,

                subject:
                    subject || null,

                date:
                    date,

                priority:
                    priority || "medium",

                description:
                    description || null

            };


            const saveBtn =
                document.getElementById(
                    "saveBtn"
                );


            saveBtn.disabled = true;

            saveBtn.textContent =
                editingTaskId
                    ? "Updating..."
                    : "Saving...";


            try {

                let response;


                /* ==========================================
                   UPDATE
                ========================================== */

                if (editingTaskId) {

                    response =
                        await fetch(
                            `${API_BASE}/api/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(editingTaskId)}`,
                            {
                                method: "PUT",

                                headers: {
                                    "Content-Type":
                                        "application/json"
                                },

                                body:
                                    JSON.stringify(
                                        payload
                                    )
                            }
                        );

                }


                /* ==========================================
                   CREATE
                ========================================== */

                else {

                    response =
                        await fetch(
                            `${API_BASE}/api/tasks`,
                            {
                                method: "POST",

                                headers: {
                                    "Content-Type":
                                        "application/json"
                                },

                                body:
                                    JSON.stringify(
                                        payload
                                    )
                            }
                        );

                }


                const data =
                    await response.json();


                if (!response.ok) {

                    throw new Error(
                        data.message ||
                        "Unable to save task."
                    );

                }


                closeModal();

                await loadTasks();

            }

            catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "Save task error:",
                    error
                );

                alert(
                    error.message ||
                    "Unable to save task."
                );

            }

            finally {

                saveBtn.disabled = false;

                saveBtn.textContent =
                    editingTaskId
                        ? "Update Task"
                        : "Save Task";

            }

        }


        /* =====================================================
           TOGGLE TASK
           
           IMPORTANT:
           Uses existing PUT route.
           No PATCH /toggle dependency.
        ===================================================== */

        async function toggleTask(id) {

            const task =
                tasks.find(
                    item =>
                        String(item.id) ===
                        String(id)
                );

            if (!task) {
                return;
            }


            const currentStatus =
                Number(task.completed) === 1;


            const payload = {

                user_email:
                    userEmail,

                name:
                    task.name,

                subject:
                    task.subject || null,

                date:
                    task.task_date ||
                    task.date,

                priority:
                    task.priority || "medium",

                description:
                    task.description || null,

                completed:
                    currentStatus
                        ? 0
                        : 1

            };


            try {

                const response =
                    await fetch(
                        `${API_BASE}/api/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
                        {
                            method: "PUT",

                            headers: {
                                "Content-Type":
                                    "application/json"
                            },

                            body:
                                JSON.stringify(
                                    payload
                                )
                        }
                    );


                const data =
                    await response.json();


                if (!response.ok) {

                    throw new Error(
                        data.message ||
                        "Unable to update task."
                    );

                }


                await loadTasks();

            }

            catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "Toggle task error:",
                    error
                );

                alert(
                    error.message ||
                    "Unable to update task."
                );

            }

        }


        /* =====================================================
           DELETE TASK
        ===================================================== */

        async function deleteTask(id) {

            const task =
                tasks.find(
                    item =>
                        String(item.id) ===
                        String(id)
                );

            if (!task) {
                return;
            }


            const confirmed =
                confirm(
                    `Delete "${task.name}"?`
                );


            if (!confirmed) {
                return;
            }


            try {

                const response =
                    await fetch(
                        `${API_BASE}/api/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
                        {
                            method: "DELETE",

                            headers: {
                                "Content-Type":
                                    "application/json"
                            },

                            body:
                                JSON.stringify({
                                    user_email:
                                        userEmail
                                })
                        }
                    );


                const data =
                    await response.json();


                if (!response.ok) {

                    throw new Error(
                        data.message ||
                        "Unable to delete task."
                    );

                }


                await loadTasks();

            }

            catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    "Delete task error:",
                    error
                );

                alert(
                    error.message ||
                    "Unable to delete task."
                );

            }

        }


        /* =====================================================
           CLOSE MODAL
        ===================================================== */

        function closeModal() {

            document.getElementById(
                "taskModal"
            ).classList.remove("show");

            editingTaskId = null;

        }


        /* =====================================================
           FILTER BUTTONS
        ===================================================== */

        document.querySelectorAll(
            ".filter-btn"
        ).forEach(button => {

            button.addEventListener(
                "click",
                () => {

                    document.querySelectorAll(
                        ".filter-btn"
                    ).forEach(btn => {

                        btn.classList.remove(
                            "active"
                        );

                    });


                    button.classList.add(
                        "active"
                    );


                    currentFilter =
                        button.dataset.filter;


                    renderTasks();

                }
            );

        });


        /* =====================================================
           MODAL EVENTS
        ===================================================== */

        document.getElementById(
            "addTaskBtn"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            openAddModal
        );


        document.getElementById(
            "closeModalBtn"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            closeModal
        );


        document.getElementById(
            "cancelBtn"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            closeModal
        );


        document.getElementById(
            "taskForm"
        ).addEventListener(
            "submit",
            saveTask
        );


        document.getElementById(
            "taskModal"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            event => {

                if (
                    event.target.id ===
                    "taskModal"
                ) {

                    closeModal();

                }

            }
        );


        /* =====================================================
           MOBILE MENU
        ===================================================== */

        document.getElementById(
            "mobileMenuBtn"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            () => {

                document
                    .getElementById("sidebar")
                    .classList.toggle(
                        "mobile-open"
                    );

            }
        );


        /* =====================================================
           TOP NAV
        ===================================================== */

        document.getElementById(
            "calendarBtn"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            () => {

                window.location.href =
                    "calendar.html";

            }
        );


        document.getElementById(
            "notificationBtn"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            () => {

                window.location.href =
                    "notifications.html";

            }
        );


        /* =====================================================
           LOGOUT
        ===================================================== */

        document.getElementById(
            "logoutBtn"
        ).addEventListener(
            "click",
            event => {

                event.preventDefault();


                const confirmed =
                    confirm(
                        "Are you sure you want to logout?"
                    );


                if (!confirmed) {
                    return;
                }


                sessionStorage.removeItem(
                    "dueMateUser"
                );

                sessionStorage.removeItem(
                    "currentUser"
                );

                sessionStorage.removeItem(
                    "loggedInUser"
                );

                sessionStorage.removeItem(
                    "user"
                );

                sessionStorage.removeItem(
                    "userEmail"
                );

                sessionStorage.removeItem(
                    "email"
                );


                window.location.href =
                    "index.html";

            }
        );


        /* =====================================================
           HELPERS
        ===================================================== */

        function capitalize(value) {

            if (!value) {
                return "";
            }

            return (
                value.charAt(0).toUpperCase() +
                value.slice(1)
            );

        }


        function escapeHtml(value) {

            return String(
                value ?? ""
            )
                .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
                .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
                .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
                .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
                .replaceAll("'", "&#039;");

        }


        function escapeJs(value) {

            return String(
                value ?? ""
            )
                .replaceAll("\\", "\\\\")
                .replaceAll("'", "\\'")
                .replaceAll("\n", "\\n")
                .replaceAll("\r", "\\r");

        }


        /* =====================================================
           SYSTEM THEME CHANGE
        ===================================================== */

        if (window.matchMedia) {

            const media =
                window.matchMedia(
                    "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
                );


            media.addEventListener(
                "change",
                () => {

                    const user =
                        getCurrentUser();

                    const theme =
                        user?.theme ||
                        sessionStorage.getItem(
                            "dueMateTheme"
                        ) ||
                        "system";


                    if (theme === "system") {

                        applyTheme("system");

                    }

                }
            );

        }


        /* =====================================================
           START
        ===================================================== */

        if (userEmail) {

            loadTasks();

        }

    </script>

</body>
</html>
